# W7 — Web UX Parity Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Claude Code's interactive experience in Orin's web interface (spec §10): built-in slash commands with the same
names, a keybinding engine, the chat surfaces (message queue, three-option permission card, per-message rewind,
background tasks, status line, detailed transcript, context grid, notifications) and settings editors, plus the items
earlier waves handed to W7.

**Architecture:** The backend gains the routes the interface needs (Batch A): effective settings with provenance and
per-layer editing, hooks, agents, output styles, keybindings, trusted folders, background tasks, the command catalog and
the status line, with secrets redacted in every streamed sink. The frontend (React + TypeScript, Vitest, Playwright)
gains an API client for them, a keybinding engine with Claude Code's contexts and action names, a command registry the
composer uses, and the surfaces and editors (Batches B–E). Every new surface has a Playwright e2e test with axe and
visual snapshots (desktop dark, mobile 375 px light; active, empty and error states).

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13 / FastAPI (backend), React 19 + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind 3 (frontend), Vitest, Playwright
with @axe-core/playwright.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-18-orin-code-claude-code-full-parity-design.md` §10. Facts about Claude Code's
public behaviour (hook events, `statusLine`, `keybindings.json`, slash commands, the permission prompt) come from its
public documentation (code.claude.com/docs), read on 2026-10-20; nothing is copied from Claude Code's code or prompts.

## Global constraints

- Work on `main`; one commit per task with the message given, a blank line and `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- TDD for every task: the test first, run it and see it fail, then the code, then green.
- Tests never write to the real home folders (`tests/conftest.py` isolates the `ORIN_*` variables).
- `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` stays under 1700 lines.
- Host-file editors (settings, hooks, agents, output styles, keybindings, trusted folders) exist only where the
  instance may touch host folders (`InstanceCapabilities.host_folders`, the local profile); a shared server answers 404
  (per-profile settings are W8). An `orin code` token may read them but never write.
- Interface text stays in Brazilian Portuguese, as the rest of the interface; command names, setting keys and action
  identifiers keep Claude Code's English names.
- Frontend: no new runtime dependency without a reason written in the task; dependencies are pinned (Batch B).

## Batches

- **Batch A — Backend routes (Tasks 1–6):** secret redaction in sinks (W0-R11); the settings API; process identity and
  the background tasks routes; the status line and the command catalog; the UserPromptExpansion and Notification hook
  events; W6-R9/R15 (CLI data retention and owner-only folders).
- **Batch B — Frontend foundations (Tasks 7–11):** pinned dependencies and the aligned version; the API client for
  Batch A; the keybinding engine (contexts, browser and Electron defaults, `keybindings.json`, validator); built-in
  slash commands in the composer and the chat.
- **Batch C — Backend for the chat surfaces (Tasks 12–15):** fork and rename a conversation; shell turns (`!`);
  per-message rewind of the conversation and of both; `@server:uri` MCP resource mentions.
- **Batch D — Chat surfaces:** the `!` shell mode in the composer; the message queue with "send now"; the three-option
  permission card; the per-message rewind picker (code, conversation, both); background tasks panel; status line;
  detailed transcript (`ctrl+o`); `/context` grid; notifications; the commands those surfaces enable.
- **Batch E — Settings editors:** permissions per layer with provenance; hooks; MCP per scope (file servers, the
  approval of a project's servers); agents; output styles; keybindings; trusted folders; plugin install dialog details.
- **Batch F — Remaining inherited items and the W7 close.**

Each batch's tasks follow below as the batch is planned.

## Decisions recorded while planning (the ledger repeats them)

1. **Host-file editors are local-profile only** (fail closed on a shared server); `orin code`'s token cannot write them.
2. **Redaction (W0-R11):** the activity log (and so its SSE stream and the interface) and HTTP hooks redact secrets in
   every string (`NAME=value` with a secret word in the name, `Bearer …`, `--password x`, URL passwords, private keys,
   known token shapes); local command hooks and the `orin code` transcript keep raw values, as in Claude Code.
3. **The status line** runs only from a trusted folder, never while `disableAllHooks` is on, only from managed policy
   under `allowManagedHooksOnly`, with a 5 s timeout, at most 5 lines of 400 characters (ANSI kept), and at most one
   run per conversation at a time (a repeat within 1 s reuses the answer). Its stdin carries the documented fields Orin
   knows (`session_id`, `session_name`, `cwd`, `model`, `workspace`, `version`, `output_style`, `cost`,
   `context_window` token totals); the others are omitted.
4. **StatusLine and FileSuggestion are settings, not hook events** (Claude Code's documentation); MessageDisplay (a hook
   that rewrites streamed text for display) is not implemented: W8.
5. **Background shells record their process creation time**; the tasks panel stops a shell only when the pid still
   names that process (W6-R3's lesson), and an entry written before W7 is shown but never stopped.
6. **The session index of `orin code` stays without an ACL of its own** (W6-R15 covers the transcripts and host
   requests, which hold tool data; the index holds folder names and ids, and restricting it on every write made the
   four-process test slow and flaky).

---

## Batch A — Backend routes (Tasks 1–6)

**Every "Create" below is a new file: if a file of that name exists, stop and report — never overwrite it.**

### Task 1: Secrets are redacted in every streamed sink (W0-R11)

A new module `agentos/redaction.py` (`redact_text`, `redact_value`) replaces secret-looking parts with `[REDACTED]`: `NAME=value` where the name holds a secret word (`GITHUB_TOKEN=…`, `db_password: …`), `Bearer …`, `--password x`, a URL's password, private key blocks and known token shapes (GitHub, `sk-`, Slack, AWS, Google, GitLab, JWT). The activity sanitizer (`sanitize_public_mapping` and `sanitize_summary`, which every activity event goes through, so the SSE stream and the interface too) redacts every string; an HTTP hook receives the event through `redact_value` (values inside `env` and `headers`, and under secret keys, are hidden whole). Local command hooks and the `orin code` transcript keep raw values, as in Claude Code.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/agentos/redaction.py`, `tests/unit/agentic/test_w7_sink_redaction.py`, `tests/unit/test_redaction.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/models.py`, `src/agentos/hooks/runners.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `agentos.redaction.redact_text(str) -> str`, `redact_value(obj) -> obj`, `is_secret_key(str)`, `REDACTED`.

Create `tests/unit/agentic/test_w7_sink_redaction.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""W7 (W0-R11): a token in a tool's command never reaches the activity log, its SSE stream or an HTTP hook."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import httpx

from agentos.agentic.events import AgentActivityEvent, AgentActivityEventType
from agentos.agentic.lifecycle import HookEvent
from agentos.hooks.config import parse_hook_mapping
from agentos.hooks.runners import HttpHookRunner

TOKEN = "ghp_0123456789abcdefghijABCDEF"


def _event(summary: str, payload: dict) -> AgentActivityEvent:
    from datetime import UTC, datetime

    fields = AgentActivityEvent.__dataclass_fields__
    values = {
        "event_id": "e1", "conversation_id": "c1", "turn_id": "t1", "execution_id": "x1", "user_id": "u1",
        "agent_id": "a1", "parent_agent_id": None,
        "event_type": AgentActivityEventType.TOOL_STARTED, "summary": summary, "payload": payload,
        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC), "sequence": 1,
    }
    return AgentActivityEvent(**{key: value for key, value in values.items() if key in fields})


def test_the_activity_event_redacts_a_token_in_the_command_and_summary() -> None:
    event = _event(f"Ran GITHUB_TOKEN={TOKEN} git push", {"command": f"GITHUB_TOKEN={TOKEN} git push", "label": "git push"})
    assert TOKEN not in event.summary and TOKEN not in json.dumps(dict(event.payload))
    assert event.payload["label"] == "git push"


def test_an_http_hook_receives_the_event_without_its_secrets() -> None:
    seen: list[bytes] = []

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        seen.append(request.content)
        return httpx.Response(200, json={})

    specs, errors = parse_hook_mapping({"PreToolUse": [{"hooks": [{"type": "http", "url": "https://hooks.example.com/x"}]}]}, "user")
    assert not errors
    runner = HttpHookRunner(client_factory=lambda: httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)))
    runner.run(specs[0], {"hook_event_name": HookEvent.PRE_TOOL_USE.value, "tool_input": {"command": f"TOKEN={TOKEN} deploy"}})
    assert seen and TOKEN.encode() not in seen[0] and b"deploy" in seen[0]
```

```python
"""W7 (W0-R11): secrets never leave through a sink."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from agentos.redaction import REDACTED, redact_text, redact_value


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text, secret", [
    ("GITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_0123456789abcdefghijABCD curl https://api.github.com", "ghp_0123456789abcdefghijABCD"),
    ("export DB_PASSWORD='hunter2 two' && run", "hunter2 two"),
    ('{"api_key": "abc123xyz"}', "abc123xyz"),
    ("curl -H 'Authorization: Bearer abcdefghijklmnop' x", "abcdefghijklmnop"),
    ("mysql --password=s3cr3t -u root", "s3cr3t"),
    ("git clone https://carlos:tok3nvalue@github.com/x/y", "tok3nvalue"),
    ("key sk-ant-api03-ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUV in the text", "sk-ant-api03-ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUV"),
    ("AWS AKIAABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP here", "AKIAABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP"),
    ("-----BEGIN RSA PRIVATE KEY-----\nMIIabc\n-----END RSA PRIVATE KEY-----", "MIIabc"),
    ("jwt eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1.eyJzdWIiOiIx.SflKxwRJSMeKKF2QT4", "SflKxwRJSMeKKF2QT4"),
])
def test_secrets_are_redacted(text: str, secret: str) -> None:
    redacted = redact_text(text)
    assert secret not in redacted and REDACTED in redacted


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["git status", "ls -la src/agentos", "pytest -q tests/unit", "echo hello world", "path=src/a.py"])
def test_ordinary_text_is_kept(text: str) -> None:
    assert redact_text(text) == text


def test_mappings_hide_secret_keys_env_and_headers() -> None:
    value = {
        "command": "TOKEN=abcdefgh123 make",
        "env": {"PATH": "/usr/bin", "ANYTHING": "visible?"},
        "headers": {"X-Custom": "v"},
        "nested": [{"client_secret": "s"}, {"name": "kept"}],
        "enabled": True,
    }
    result = redact_value(value)
    assert "abcdefgh123" not in result["command"]
    assert result["env"] == {"PATH": REDACTED, "ANYTHING": REDACTED}
    assert result["headers"] == {"X-Custom": REDACTED}
    assert result["nested"] == [{"client_secret": REDACTED}, {"name": "kept"}]
    assert result["enabled"] is True and value["env"]["PATH"] == "/usr/bin"  # the input is not changed


def test_too_deep_is_redacted() -> None:
    deep: dict = {}
    current = deep
    for _ in range(40):
        current["x"] = {}
        current = current["x"]
    current["y"] = "z"
    assert REDACTED in repr(redact_value(deep))
```

Create `src/agentos/redaction.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""Secret redaction for everything Orin streams or sends away (W0-R11, W7).

Local command hooks and the `orin code` transcript get raw values, as in Claude Code. What leaves through a sink that
other software reads (the activity log and its SSE stream, HTTP hooks, the settings API) goes through these helpers.
They over-redact on purpose: a word like ``author=`` may lose its value, a token never survives.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

REDACTED = "[REDACTED]"
MAX_DEPTH = 32

_SECRET_WORD = r"(?:token|secret|passw(?:or)?d|passphrase|api[_-]?key|access[_-]?key|private[_-]?key|client[_-]?secret|credential|cookie|session[_-]?id|auth)"
# NAME=value, NAME: value, "NAME": "value", where NAME contains a secret word (GITHUB_TOKEN, db_password, X-Api-Key).
_ASSIGNMENT = re.compile(
    rf"(?i)(?P<name>[\"']?[A-Za-z0-9_.-]*{_SECRET_WORD}[A-Za-z0-9_.-]*[\"']?)(?P<sep>\s*[:=]\s*)"
    r"(?P<value>\"[^\"\r\n]*\"|'[^'\r\n]*'|[^\s,;&|)\]}]+)"
)
# --password x, --token=x, -p is not covered (too ambiguous).
_FLAG = re.compile(rf"(?i)(?P<flag>--?[A-Za-z0-9-]*{_SECRET_WORD}[A-Za-z0-9-]*)(?P<sep>[= ]+)(?P<value>\"[^\"]*\"|'[^']*'|\S+)")
_BEARER = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(bearer|basic|token)\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+/=-]{8,}")
_URL_PASSWORD = re.compile(r"(?i)\b([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://[^/\s:@]+):[^@\s/]+@")
_PRIVATE_KEY = re.compile(r"-----BEGIN [^-]*PRIVATE KEY-----.*?(?:-----END [^-]*PRIVATE KEY-----|$)", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
_KNOWN_TOKENS = re.compile(
    r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9])(?:"
    r"gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}|"
    r"sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}|xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}|"
    r"AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}|"
    r"eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{5,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{5,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{5,}"
    r")"
)
_SECRET_KEY = re.compile(rf"(?i){_SECRET_WORD}")
# Mappings whose every value is secret whatever its key (environment blocks, HTTP headers).
_SECRET_CONTAINERS = frozenset({"env", "headers", "environment"})


def redact_text(value: str) -> str:
    """``value`` with every secret-looking part replaced by ``[REDACTED]``."""
    if not value:
        return value
    text = _PRIVATE_KEY.sub(REDACTED, value)
    text = _URL_PASSWORD.sub(lambda match: f"{match.group(1)}:{REDACTED}@", text)
    text = _BEARER.sub(lambda match: f"{match.group(1)} {REDACTED}", text)
    text = _ASSIGNMENT.sub(lambda match: f"{match.group('name')}{match.group('sep')}{REDACTED}", text)
    text = _FLAG.sub(lambda match: f"{match.group('flag')}{match.group('sep')}{REDACTED}", text)
    return _KNOWN_TOKENS.sub(REDACTED, text)


def is_secret_key(key: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_SECRET_KEY.search(str(key)))


def redact_value(value: Any, *, _depth: int = 0, _secret: bool = False) -> Any:
    """A copy of a JSON-like value: strings redacted, values under secret keys (and inside ``env``/``headers``)
    replaced entirely. Deeper than ``MAX_DEPTH`` is replaced too (fail closed)."""
    if _depth > MAX_DEPTH:
        return REDACTED
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        result: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, item in value.items():
            name = str(key)
            hidden = _secret or is_secret_key(name)
            if name.lower() in _SECRET_CONTAINERS and isinstance(item, Mapping):
                result[name] = {str(inner): REDACTED for inner in item}
            elif hidden and not isinstance(item, (Mapping, list, tuple)):
                result[name] = REDACTED if item not in (None, "", True, False) else item
            else:
                result[name] = redact_value(item, _depth=_depth + 1, _secret=hidden)
        return result
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [redact_value(item, _depth=_depth + 1, _secret=_secret) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return REDACTED if _secret and value else redact_text(value)
    return value


__all__ = ["REDACTED", "is_secret_key", "redact_text", "redact_value"]
```

### Task 2: The settings API

`api/settings_routes.py`, registered by the gateway, local profile only (`host_folders`; 404 on a shared server), readable but never writable with `orin code`'s token: `GET /v1/settings/effective?conversation_id=` (the merged values and the layers, redacted; `provenance`; every element of `permissions.allow/ask/deny/additionalDirectories` with its layer and file, highest precedence first, a repeat marked `duplicate` — W0-R18; the files of each layer and the writable one); `PUT /v1/settings/permissions` (add or remove one rule in the user, project or local layer; project and local need a conversation's folder; a rule is checked with the engine's parser); `GET`/`PUT /v1/settings/hooks` (the effective hooks and each writable layer's raw `hooks`; a save is parsed first, callback hooks refused); `GET /v1/settings/agents`, `PUT`/`DELETE /v1/settings/agents/{name}` (Orin's own agent folders: `~/.orin/agents`, the project's `.orin/agents`; the file is parsed back before it replaces anything; builtin names refused; tools are names only); `GET`/`PUT /v1/settings/output-styles`; `GET`/`PUT /v1/settings/keybindings` (`~/.orin/keybindings.json` and Claude Code's, read-only; a save must be JSON with a list of bindings, 64 KB at most — the interface validates the rest); `GET /v1/settings/trusted-folders` and `POST /v1/settings/trusted-folders/revoke`. A refusal answers 422 with `details.problems`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/agentos/api/settings_routes.py`, `tests/unit/api/test_w7_settings_api.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/api/gateway.py`, `src/agentos/permissions/trust.py`, `tests/integration/api/test_profile_isolation.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `register_settings_routes(app, services, principal_for, conversation_root)`, `SettingsRejected(code, problems)`; `WorkspaceTrustStore.trusted_roots(user_id)`, `.revoke_key(user_id, key)`.

Create `tests/unit/api/test_w7_settings_api.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""W7: the settings API the interface's editors use (host files: local profile only)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from agentos.agentic.settings import AgentRuntimeSettingsStore
from agentos.api.gateway import ApiServices, create_app
from agentos.api.security import AuthenticatedPrincipal, InMemorySecurityService
from agentos.configuration.capabilities import InstanceCapabilities
from agentos.configuration.mode import RuntimeMode
from agentos.settings.paths import orin_managed_dir, orin_user_dir

AUTH = {"Authorization": "Bearer test-pat"}
CLI = {"Authorization": "Bearer cli-token"}


class _Conversations:
    def get(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {"conversation_id": conversation_id, "title": "T", "state": "idle", "provider": "openai", "model_id": "m",
                "messages": [], "turns": [], "activities": [], "activity_cursor": "0"}


def _client(tmp_path: Path, mode: RuntimeMode = RuntimeMode.LOCAL) -> TestClient:
    security = InMemorySecurityService()
    security.add_pat("test-pat", AuthenticatedPrincipal(user_id="local-user", credential_ref="pat-1", scopes=frozenset({"api"})))
    security.add_pat("cli-token", AuthenticatedPrincipal(user_id="local-user", credential_ref="cli", scopes=frozenset({"api"}),
                                                         credential_kind="cli_token"))
    services = ApiServices(
        security=security, conversation_application=_Conversations(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        agentic_runtime=AgentRuntimeSettingsStore(tmp_path / "runtime.json"), workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspaces",
        capabilities=InstanceCapabilities.for_mode(mode),
    )
    return TestClient(create_app(services))


def _write(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")


def test_effective_settings_carry_provenance_per_rule_and_hide_secrets(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    _write(orin_user_dir() / "settings.json", {"permissions": {"allow": ["Bash(git status)", "Read"]}, "env": {"API_TOKEN": "s3cret"}})
    _write(orin_managed_dir() / "managed-settings.json", {"permissions": {"deny": ["Bash(rm:*)"], "allow": ["Read"]}})
    body = _client(tmp_path).get("/v1/settings/effective", headers=AUTH).json()
    assert "s3cret" not in json.dumps(body)
    allow = body["permissions"]["allow"]
    assert [(item["rule"], item["layer"], item["duplicate"]) for item in allow] == [
        ("Read", "managed", False), ("Bash(git status)", "user", False), ("Read", "user", True)]
    assert body["permissions"]["deny"][0]["layer"] == "managed"
    assert body["writable"]["user"].endswith("settings.json") and body["writable"]["project"] is None


def test_a_rule_is_added_and_removed_in_the_chosen_layer(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    added = client.put("/v1/settings/permissions", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "kind": "deny", "rule": "WebFetch", "op": "add"})
    assert added.status_code == 200 and added.json()["permissions"]["deny"][0]["rule"] == "WebFetch"
    assert json.loads((orin_user_dir() / "settings.json").read_text())["permissions"]["deny"] == ["WebFetch"]
    removed = client.put("/v1/settings/permissions", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "kind": "deny", "rule": "WebFetch", "op": "remove"})
    assert removed.json()["permissions"]["deny"] == []
    assert "permissions" not in json.loads((orin_user_dir() / "settings.json").read_text())


def test_project_and_local_rules_need_a_conversation_and_land_in_its_folder(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    refused = client.put("/v1/settings/permissions", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "project", "kind": "allow", "rule": "Read", "op": "add"})
    assert refused.status_code == 422 and refused.json()["error"]["code"] == "project_required"
    saved = client.put("/v1/settings/permissions", headers=AUTH,
                       json={"conversation_id": "c1", "layer": "project", "kind": "allow", "rule": "Bash(npm test)", "op": "add"})
    assert saved.status_code == 200
    root = Path(client.get("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers=AUTH).json()["path"])
    assert json.loads((root / ".orin" / "settings.json").read_text())["permissions"]["allow"] == ["Bash(npm test)"]


def test_an_invalid_rule_is_refused_with_its_reason(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    response = _client(tmp_path).put("/v1/settings/permissions", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "kind": "allow", "rule": "Bash(", "op": "add"})
    assert response.status_code == 422 and response.json()["error"]["details"]["problems"]


def test_hooks_are_listed_and_saved_after_validation(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    hooks = {"PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Bash", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo checked"}]}]}
    saved = client.put("/v1/settings/hooks", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "hooks": hooks})
    assert saved.status_code == 200
    body = saved.json()
    assert body["editable"]["user"]["hooks"] == hooks
    assert [(item["event"], item["matcher"], item["source"]) for item in body["effective"]] == [("PreToolUse", "Bash", "user")]
    bad = client.put("/v1/settings/hooks", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "hooks": {"PreToolUse": [{"hooks": [{"type": "nope"}]}]}})
    assert bad.status_code == 422
    callback = client.put("/v1/settings/hooks", headers=AUTH,
                          json={"layer": "user", "hooks": {"PreToolUse": [{"hooks": [{"type": "callback", "callback_id": "x"}]}]}})
    assert callback.status_code == 422
    assert json.loads((orin_user_dir() / "settings.json").read_text())["hooks"] == hooks  # the refused saves changed nothing


def test_an_agent_is_written_read_back_and_deleted(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    body = {"scope": "user", "description": "Reviews: carefully", "prompt": "Review the diff.", "tools": ["Read", "Grep", "Bash"],
            "model": "sonnet", "max_turns": 5, "effort": "high"}
    saved = client.put("/v1/settings/agents/code-reviewer", headers=AUTH, json=body)
    assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
    agent = next(item for item in saved.json()["agents"] if item["name"] == "code-reviewer")
    assert agent["editable"] and agent["description"] == "Reviews: carefully" and agent["prompt"] == "Review the diff."
    assert agent["tools"] == ["Read", "Grep", "Bash"]
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/agents/x", headers=AUTH, json={**body, "tools": ["Bash(git diff:*)"]}).status_code == 422
    assert agent["model"] == "sonnet" and agent["effort"] == "high"
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/agents/verifier", headers=AUTH, json=body).status_code == 422  # a builtin name
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/agents/Bad Name", headers=AUTH, json=body).status_code == 422
    deleted = client.delete("/v1/settings/agents/code-reviewer?scope=user", headers=AUTH)
    assert deleted.status_code == 200 and all(item["name"] != "code-reviewer" for item in deleted.json()["agents"])
    assert client.delete("/v1/settings/agents/code-reviewer?scope=user", headers=AUTH).status_code == 404


def test_output_style_choice_is_checked_and_saved(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    listed = client.get("/v1/settings/output-styles", headers=AUTH).json()
    assert {"default", "explanatory", "learning"} <= {item["key"] for item in listed["styles"]} and listed["active"] == "default"
    chosen = client.put("/v1/settings/output-styles", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "style": "Explanatory"})
    assert chosen.status_code == 200 and chosen.json()["active"] == "explanatory" and chosen.json()["active_layer"] == "user"
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/output-styles", headers=AUTH, json={"layer": "user", "style": "nope"}).status_code == 422


def test_keybindings_are_stored_only_when_they_are_a_list(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    text = json.dumps({"bindings": [{"context": "Chat", "bindings": {"ctrl+k": "chat:submit"}}]})
    saved = client.put("/v1/settings/keybindings", headers=AUTH, json={"text": text})
    assert saved.status_code == 200 and saved.json()["orin"]["text"] == text
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/keybindings", headers=AUTH, json={"text": "{nope"}).status_code == 422
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/keybindings", headers=AUTH, json={"text": '{"bindings": 3}'}).status_code == 422
    assert (orin_user_dir() / "keybindings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == text


def test_trusted_folders_are_listed_and_revoked(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers={**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "t1"})
    folders = client.get("/v1/settings/trusted-folders", headers=AUTH).json()["folders"]
    assert len(folders) == 1
    revoked = client.post("/v1/settings/trusted-folders/revoke", headers=AUTH, json={"path": folders[0]})
    assert revoked.status_code == 200 and revoked.json()["folders"] == []
    assert client.post("/v1/settings/trusted-folders/revoke", headers=AUTH, json={"path": folders[0]}).status_code == 404


def test_a_shared_server_has_no_settings_routes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path, RuntimeMode.SERVER)
    assert client.get("/v1/settings/effective", headers=AUTH).status_code == 404
    assert client.put("/v1/settings/keybindings", headers=AUTH, json={"text": "[]"}).status_code == 404
```

Create `src/agentos/api/settings_routes.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""Settings the person edits from the interface (W7): effective settings with provenance, permission rules per layer,
hooks, agents, output styles, keybindings and trusted folders.

Every route reads or writes files on the host (``~/.orin``, a workspace's ``.orin``), so they exist only where the
instance may touch host folders (the local profile); a shared server answers 404 (per-profile settings are W8). An
``orin code`` token may read but never write here. Values that leave through this API are redacted except where the
person edits them (the raw rules and hooks of a writable layer).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.concurrency import run_in_threadpool
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from agentos.redaction import redact_value

from .contracts import ApplicationNotFoundError
from .security import AuthenticatedPrincipal, AuthorizationError

WRITABLE_LAYERS = ("user", "project", "local")
RULE_LISTS = ("allow", "ask", "deny", "additionalDirectories")
MAX_KEYBINDINGS_BYTES = 64 * 1024
MAX_AGENT_PROMPT = 40_000
_AGENT_NAME = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$")
_TOOL_NAME = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][\w.:-]*$")  # an agent file lists tool names, never permission rules


class SettingsRejected(ValueError):
    """A change the API refuses, with the reasons the editor shows."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, problems: list[str]) -> None:
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.problems = problems[:50]


class PermissionRuleChange(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    conversation_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)
    layer: Literal["user", "project", "local"]
    kind: Literal["allow", "ask", "deny", "additionalDirectories"]
    rule: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1000)
    op: Literal["add", "remove"]


class HooksChange(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    conversation_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)
    layer: Literal["user", "project", "local"]
    hooks: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class AgentFile(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    conversation_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)
    scope: Literal["user", "project"]
    description: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1024)
    prompt: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=MAX_AGENT_PROMPT)
    tools: list[str] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=128)
    disallowed_tools: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=128)
    model: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=120)
    permission_mode: Literal["default", "acceptEdits", "plan", "dontAsk", "bypassPermissions", "auto"] | None = None
    max_turns: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=500)
    effort: Literal["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max"] | None = None


class OutputStyleChange(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    conversation_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)
    layer: Literal["user", "local"]
    style: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)


class KeybindingsChange(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    text: str = Field(max_length=MAX_KEYBINDINGS_BYTES)


class TrustedFolderChange(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    path: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4096)


def _rejected(error: SettingsRejected) -> JSONResponse:
    return JSONResponse(
        {"error": {"code": error.code, "category": "VALIDATION", "message_key": error.code, "retryable": False,
                   "details": {"problems": error.problems}}},
        status_code=422,
    )


def _settings(root: Path | None, trusted: bool):
    from agentos.settings import load_effective_settings

    return load_effective_settings(root, trusted=trusted)


def _rule_entries(settings, key: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Every element of ``permissions.<key>`` with its layer and file, highest precedence first (W0-R18). A rule a
    higher layer already lists is kept, marked ``duplicate``."""
    seen: set[str] = set()
    entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for layer in reversed(settings.layers):
        values = (layer.data.get("permissions") or {}).get(key) if isinstance(layer.data.get("permissions"), dict) else None
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            continue
        for value in values:
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                continue
            text = value.strip()
            entries.append({"rule": text, "layer": layer.name, "file": str(layer.path) if layer.path else None,
                            "duplicate": text in seen})
            seen.add(text)
    return entries


def _file_report(paths, layer: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [{"path": str(path), "exists": path.is_file()} for path in paths.files_for(layer)]


def effective_report(root: Path | None, trusted: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from agentos.settings.paths import SettingsPaths

    settings = _settings(root, trusted)
    paths = SettingsPaths(root)
    writable: dict[str, str | None] = {}
    for layer in WRITABLE_LAYERS:
        try:
            writable[layer] = str(paths.writable_file(layer))
        except ValueError:
            writable[layer] = None
    return {
        "project_root": str(root) if root is not None else None,
        "trusted": trusted,
        "values": redact_value(dict(settings.data)),
        "provenance": dict(settings.provenance),
        "layers": [{"name": layer.name, "file": str(layer.path) if layer.path else None, "data": redact_value(dict(layer.data))}
                   for layer in settings.layers],
        "files": {layer: _file_report(paths, layer) for layer in ("managed", *WRITABLE_LAYERS)},
        "writable": writable,
        "permissions": {key: _rule_entries(settings, key) for key in RULE_LISTS},
        "errors": [{"file": error.path, "message": error.message} for error in settings.errors],
        "untrusted_ignored": list(settings.untrusted_ignored),
    }


def change_permission_rule(root: Path | None, change: PermissionRuleChange) -> None:
    from agentos.permissions.rules import parse_rule
    from agentos.settings.paths import SettingsPaths
    from agentos.settings.writer import update_settings_file

    rule = change.rule.strip()
    if change.kind == "additionalDirectories":
        if "\n" in rule or "\x00" in rule:
            raise SettingsRejected("invalid_directory", ["a folder is one line"])
    elif change.op == "add" and parse_rule(rule, kind=change.kind, source=change.layer) is None:
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_rule", [f"'{rule[:200]}' is not a permission rule"])
    try:
        target = SettingsPaths(root).writable_file(change.layer)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SettingsRejected("project_required", [str(error)]) from error

    def mutate(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        permissions = data.get("permissions")
        if not isinstance(permissions, dict):
            permissions = data["permissions"] = {}
        current = permissions.get(change.kind)
        items = [item for item in current if isinstance(item, str)] if isinstance(current, list) else []
        items = [item for item in items if item.strip() != rule]
        if change.op == "add":
            items.append(rule)
        if items:
            permissions[change.kind] = items
        else:
            permissions.pop(change.kind, None)
        if not permissions:
            data.pop("permissions", None)

    update_settings_file(target, mutate)


def hooks_report(root: Path | None, trusted: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from agentos.hooks.config import load_hook_specs
    from agentos.settings.paths import SettingsPaths

    settings = _settings(root, trusted)
    specs, errors = load_hook_specs(settings)
    paths = SettingsPaths(root)
    editable: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for layer in WRITABLE_LAYERS:
        try:
            target = paths.writable_file(layer)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        try:
            data = json.loads(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) if target.is_file() else {}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            data = {}
        hooks = data.get("hooks") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
        editable[layer] = {"file": str(target), "hooks": hooks if isinstance(hooks, dict) else {}}
    return {
        "disabled": settings.get("disableAllHooks") is True,
        "effective": [{"event": spec.event.value, "matcher": spec.matcher, "type": spec.type, "source": spec.source,
                       "label": redact_value(spec.label()), "timeout": spec.timeout} for spec in specs],
        "editable": editable,
        "errors": [{"source": getattr(error, "source", ""), "message": str(getattr(error, "message", error))} for error in errors],
    }


def change_hooks(root: Path | None, change: HooksChange) -> None:
    from agentos.hooks.config import parse_hook_mapping
    from agentos.settings.paths import SettingsPaths
    from agentos.settings.writer import update_settings_file

    specs, errors = parse_hook_mapping(change.hooks, change.layer)
    problems = [str(getattr(error, "message", error)) for error in errors]
    if any(spec.type == "callback" for spec in specs):
        problems.append("callback hooks exist only for an SDK host")
    if problems:
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_hooks", problems)
    try:
        target = SettingsPaths(root).writable_file(change.layer)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SettingsRejected("project_required", [str(error)]) from error

    def mutate(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if change.hooks:
            data["hooks"] = change.hooks
        else:
            data.pop("hooks", None)

    update_settings_file(target, mutate)


def _agent_dir(scope: str, root: Path | None) -> Path:
    from agentos.settings.paths import orin_user_dir

    if scope == "user":
        return orin_user_dir() / "agents"
    if root is None:
        raise SettingsRejected("project_required", ["a project agent needs a conversation with a folder"])
    return root / ".orin" / "agents"


def agents_report(root: Path | None, trusted: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from agentos.agentic.agent_definitions import load_agent_definitions

    catalog = load_agent_definitions(workspace_root=root, trusted=trusted)
    editable_dirs = {"user": _agent_dir("user", None)}
    if root is not None:
        editable_dirs["project"] = root / ".orin" / "agents"
    agents = []
    for definition in catalog.agents.values():
        path = definition.path
        editable = path is not None and any(path.parent == folder for folder in editable_dirs.values())
        agents.append({
            "name": definition.name, "description": definition.description, "origin": definition.origin,
            "path": str(path) if path else None, "editable": editable, "prompt": definition.prompt,
            "tools": list(definition.tools) if definition.tools is not None else None,
            "disallowed_tools": list(definition.disallowed_tools), "model": definition.model,
            "permission_mode": definition.permission_mode, "max_turns": definition.max_turns, "effort": definition.effort,
        })
    return {
        "agents": sorted(agents, key=lambda item: (item["origin"] == "builtin", item["name"])),
        "errors": [{"file": file, "message": message} for file, message in catalog.errors],
        "shadowed": [{"name": name, "kept": kept, "ignored": ignored} for name, kept, ignored in catalog.shadowed],
    }


def _scalar(value: str) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)


def write_agent(root: Path | None, name: str, body: AgentFile) -> Path:
    from agentos.agentic.agent_definitions import RESERVED_NAMES, parse_agent_file

    clean = name.strip().lower()
    if not _AGENT_NAME.fullmatch(clean) or clean in RESERVED_NAMES:
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_agent_name", ["a name of lowercase letters, digits, '-' and '_' that is not a builtin agent"])
    problems = [f"'{tool[:80]}' is not a tool name" for tool in [*(body.tools or []), *body.disallowed_tools] if not _TOOL_NAME.fullmatch(tool.strip())]
    if problems:
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_agent", problems)
    lines = ["---", f"name: {clean}", f"description: {_scalar(' '.join(body.description.split()))}"]
    if body.tools is not None:
        lines.append(f"tools: {', '.join(tool.strip() for tool in body.tools) or _scalar('')}")
    if body.disallowed_tools:
        lines.append(f"disallowedTools: {', '.join(tool.strip() for tool in body.disallowed_tools)}")
    for key, value in (("model", body.model), ("permissionMode", body.permission_mode), ("effort", body.effort)):
        if value:
            lines.append(f"{key}: {_scalar(value.strip())}")
    if body.max_turns is not None:
        lines.append(f"maxTurns: {body.max_turns}")
    text = "\n".join([*lines, "---", "", body.prompt.strip(), ""])
    folder = _agent_dir(body.scope, root)
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = folder / f"{clean}.md"
    temporary = folder / f".{clean}.md.tmp"
    temporary.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        parse_agent_file(temporary, origin=body.scope)  # what Orin will read back, checked before it replaces anything
    except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
        temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_agent", [str(error)[:300]]) from error
    temporary.replace(target)
    return target


def delete_agent(root: Path | None, name: str, scope: str) -> bool:
    clean = name.strip().lower()
    if not _AGENT_NAME.fullmatch(clean):
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_agent_name", ["not an agent name"])
    target = _agent_dir(scope, root) / f"{clean}.md"
    if not target.is_file() or target.is_symlink():
        return False
    target.unlink()
    return True


def output_styles_report(root: Path | None, trusted: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from agentos.agentic.output_styles import load_output_styles

    settings = _settings(root, trusted)
    from agentos.settings.paths import orin_user_dir

    # Without a conversation there is no repository: only the built-in and personal styles.
    styles = load_output_styles(root if root is not None else orin_user_dir(), trusted=trusted and root is not None)
    active = settings.get("outputStyle")
    return {
        "styles": [{"key": key, "name": style.name, "description": style.description, "source": style.source}
                   for key, style in sorted(styles.items())],
        "active": active if isinstance(active, str) and active.strip() else "default",
        "active_layer": settings.provenance.get("outputStyle"),
    }


def change_output_style(root: Path | None, trusted: bool, change: OutputStyleChange) -> None:
    from agentos.settings.paths import SettingsPaths
    from agentos.settings.writer import update_settings_file

    key = change.style.strip().lower()
    known = {item["key"] for item in output_styles_report(root, trusted)["styles"]}
    if key not in known:
        raise SettingsRejected("unknown_output_style", [f"no output style named '{change.style[:80]}'"])
    try:
        target = SettingsPaths(root).writable_file(change.layer)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SettingsRejected("project_required", [str(error)]) from error

    def mutate(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        data["outputStyle"] = key

    update_settings_file(target, mutate)


def keybindings_files() -> dict[str, Path]:
    from agentos.settings.paths import claude_user_dir, orin_user_dir

    return {"orin": orin_user_dir() / "keybindings.json", "claude": claude_user_dir() / "keybindings.json"}


def keybindings_report() -> dict[str, Any]:
    report: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name, path in keybindings_files().items():
        entry: dict[str, Any] = {"path": str(path), "text": None, "error": None}
        try:
            if path.is_file():
                if path.stat().st_size > MAX_KEYBINDINGS_BYTES:
                    entry["error"] = f"larger than {MAX_KEYBINDINGS_BYTES} bytes"
                else:
                    entry["text"] = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        except OSError as error:
            entry["error"] = f"unreadable: {type(error).__name__}"
        report[name] = entry
    return report


def write_keybindings(change: KeybindingsChange) -> None:
    text = change.text
    try:
        data = json.loads(text) if text.strip() else []
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_keybindings", [f"not JSON: {error}"[:300]]) from error
    bindings = data.get("bindings") if isinstance(data, dict) else data
    if not isinstance(bindings, list):
        raise SettingsRejected("invalid_keybindings", ["a list of bindings, or an object with a 'bindings' list"])
    path = keybindings_files()["orin"]
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_name(".keybindings.json.tmp")
    temporary.write_text(text if text.strip() else "[]\n", encoding="utf-8")
    temporary.replace(path)


def register_settings_routes(
    app: FastAPI,
    services,
    principal_for: Callable[..., AuthenticatedPrincipal],
    conversation_root: Callable[[str, AuthenticatedPrincipal], Path],
) -> None:
    app.add_exception_handler(SettingsRejected, lambda _request, error: _rejected(error))  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def owner(request: Request, *, mutable: bool, purpose: str) -> AuthenticatedPrincipal:
        services.capabilities.require("host_folders")
        current = principal_for(request, mutable=mutable)
        services.security.authorize(current, action="settings.write" if mutable else "settings.read", resource_id=None, purpose=purpose)
        if mutable and current.credential_kind == "cli_token":
            raise AuthorizationError("orin code does not change settings through the interface's API")
        return current

    def folder_of(principal: AuthenticatedPrincipal, conversation_id: str | None) -> tuple[Path | None, bool]:
        from agentos.permissions.trust import WorkspaceTrustStore

        if not conversation_id:
            return None, False
        root = Path(conversation_root(conversation_id, principal))
        return root, WorkspaceTrustStore().is_trusted(principal.user_id, root)

    @app.get("/v1/settings/effective")
    async def effective_settings(request: Request, conversation_id: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.effective")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, conversation_id)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(effective_report, root, trusted))

    @app.put("/v1/settings/permissions")
    async def change_permissions(payload: PermissionRuleChange, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.permissions")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, payload.conversation_id)
        await run_in_threadpool(change_permission_rule, root, payload)
        report = await run_in_threadpool(effective_report, root, trusted)
        return JSONResponse({"permissions": report["permissions"]})

    @app.get("/v1/settings/hooks")
    async def list_hooks(request: Request, conversation_id: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.hooks")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, conversation_id)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(hooks_report, root, trusted))

    @app.put("/v1/settings/hooks")
    async def save_hooks(payload: HooksChange, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.hooks")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, payload.conversation_id)
        await run_in_threadpool(change_hooks, root, payload)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(hooks_report, root, trusted))

    @app.get("/v1/settings/agents")
    async def list_agents(request: Request, conversation_id: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.agents")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, conversation_id)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(agents_report, root, trusted))

    @app.put("/v1/settings/agents/{name}")
    async def save_agent(name: str, payload: AgentFile, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.agents")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, payload.conversation_id)
        path = await run_in_threadpool(write_agent, root, name, payload)
        return JSONResponse({"path": str(path), **await run_in_threadpool(agents_report, root, trusted)})

    @app.delete("/v1/settings/agents/{name}")
    async def remove_agent(name: str, request: Request, scope: Literal["user", "project"] = "user",
                           conversation_id: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.agents")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, conversation_id)
        if not await run_in_threadpool(delete_agent, root, name, scope):
            raise ApplicationNotFoundError(name)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(agents_report, root, trusted))

    @app.get("/v1/settings/output-styles")
    async def list_output_styles(request: Request, conversation_id: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.output_styles")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, conversation_id)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(output_styles_report, root, trusted))

    @app.put("/v1/settings/output-styles")
    async def choose_output_style(payload: OutputStyleChange, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.output_styles")
        root, trusted = folder_of(principal, payload.conversation_id)
        await run_in_threadpool(change_output_style, root, trusted, payload)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(output_styles_report, root, trusted))

    @app.get("/v1/settings/keybindings")
    async def read_keybindings(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.keybindings")
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(keybindings_report))

    @app.put("/v1/settings/keybindings")
    async def save_keybindings(payload: KeybindingsChange, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.keybindings")
        await run_in_threadpool(write_keybindings, payload)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(keybindings_report))

    @app.get("/v1/settings/trusted-folders")
    async def list_trusted_folders(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        from agentos.permissions.trust import WorkspaceTrustStore

        principal = owner(request, mutable=False, purpose="settings.trusted_folders")
        folders = await run_in_threadpool(WorkspaceTrustStore().trusted_roots, principal.user_id)
        return JSONResponse({"folders": folders})

    @app.post("/v1/settings/trusted-folders/revoke")
    async def revoke_trusted_folder(payload: TrustedFolderChange, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        from agentos.permissions.trust import WorkspaceTrustStore

        principal = owner(request, mutable=True, purpose="settings.trusted_folders")
        store = WorkspaceTrustStore()
        if payload.path not in await run_in_threadpool(store.trusted_roots, principal.user_id):
            raise ApplicationNotFoundError("trusted folder")
        await run_in_threadpool(store.revoke_key, principal.user_id, payload.path)
        return JSONResponse({"folders": await run_in_threadpool(store.trusted_roots, principal.user_id)})


__all__ = ["SettingsRejected", "register_settings_routes"]
```

### Task 3: Process identity, and the background tasks routes

`agentos/process_identity.py` takes `process_created`, `same_process` and a verified kill out of `orin code`'s background runs (W6-R3) so others can use them; `commands/background.py` delegates to it (same behaviour). The Bash tool's background shells record `created` in `.orin/logs/processes.json`. `api/conversation_routes.py`: `GET /v1/conversations/{id}/tasks` lists the workspace's background shells (command and log tail redacted; a log path outside the workspace is not read; `running` only when the pid still names the recorded process, `unknown` for an entry without a creation time) and `POST .../tasks/{task_id}/stop` stops a shell only when it is still that process (the `shell` capability). Agents and teammates come from `/overview`.

- Create: `src/agentos/api/conversation_routes.py`, `src/agentos/process_identity.py`, `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_w7_shell_manifest.py`, `tests/unit/api/test_w7_tasks_api.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/shell/bash.py`, `src/agentos/api/gateway.py`, `src/agentos/cli_code/commands/background.py`, `tests/integration/api/test_profile_isolation.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `process_identity.process_created(pid)`, `same_process(pid, created)`, `kill_verified(pid, created, *, group_only=False)`; `register_conversation_routes(app, services, principal_for, conversation_root)`, `shell_tasks(root)`, `stop_shell(root, task_id)`.

```python
"""W7: a background shell's manifest entry carries its creation time, so the tasks panel can stop only that process."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace
from agentos.process_identity import process_created


def test_a_background_shell_records_its_creation_time(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "chat_abc"))
    started = toolset.invoke("run_command", {"command": "python -c \"import time; time.sleep(30)\"", "background": True})
    assert started.status == "succeeded"
    pid = started.payload["pid"]
    try:
        manifest = json.loads((Path(toolset.workspace.root) / ".orin" / "logs" / "processes.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert manifest[str(pid)]["created"] is not None
        assert manifest[str(pid)]["created"] == process_created(pid)
    finally:
        toolset.invoke("stop_process", {"pid": pid})
```

```python
"""W7: the background tasks panel lists the workspace's shells and stops only the process a record names."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.process_identity import process_created

from tests.unit.api.test_w7_settings_api import AUTH, _client

SLEEP = [sys.executable, "-c", "import time; time.sleep(60)"]


def _root(client) -> Path:
    return Path(client.get("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers=AUTH).json()["path"])


def _manifest(root: Path, entries: dict) -> None:
    path = root / ".orin" / "logs" / "processes.json"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(entries), encoding="utf-8")


def test_shells_are_listed_with_a_state_that_follows_the_creation_time(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    root = _root(client)
    live = subprocess.Popen(SLEEP)
    try:
        (root / ".orin" / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (root / ".orin" / "logs" / "a.log").write_text("ready on port 3000\nGITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_0123456789abcdefghijABCD\n", encoding="utf-8")
        _manifest(root, {
            str(live.pid): {"command": "npm run dev", "log_path": ".orin/logs/a.log", "started_at": "2026-10-20T10:00:00+00:00",
                            "created": process_created(live.pid)},
            "999999": {"command": "old", "started_at": "2026-10-19T10:00:00+00:00"},  # written before W7: no creation time
            str(live.pid + 0) + "0": {"command": "reused", "created": 1, "log_path": "../../outside.txt"},
        })
        tasks = {item["command"]: item for item in client.get("/v1/conversations/c1/tasks", headers=AUTH).json()["tasks"]}
        assert tasks["npm run dev"]["state"] == "running" and tasks["npm run dev"]["stoppable"] is True
        assert "ready on port 3000" in tasks["npm run dev"]["log_tail"] and "ghp_" not in tasks["npm run dev"]["log_tail"]
        assert tasks["old"]["state"] == "unknown" and tasks["old"]["stoppable"] is False
        assert tasks["reused"]["state"] == "finished" and tasks["reused"]["log_tail"] == ""
    finally:
        live.kill()


def test_stop_kills_only_a_verified_process(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    root = _root(client)
    live = subprocess.Popen(SLEEP)
    bystander = subprocess.Popen(SLEEP)
    try:
        _manifest(root, {
            str(live.pid): {"command": "server", "created": process_created(live.pid)},
            str(bystander.pid): {"command": "reused pid", "created": 12345},
        })
        headers = {**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "s1"}
        refused = client.post(f"/v1/conversations/c1/tasks/shell:{bystander.pid}/stop", headers=headers)
        assert refused.status_code == 200 and refused.json()["stopped"] is False
        time.sleep(0.3)
        assert bystander.poll() is None
        stopped = client.post(f"/v1/conversations/c1/tasks/shell:{live.pid}/stop", headers=headers)
        assert stopped.json()["stopped"] is True
        assert live.wait(timeout=20) is not None
        assert client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/tasks/shell:1/stop", headers=headers).status_code == 404
        assert client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/tasks/agent:x/stop", headers=headers).status_code == 422
    finally:
        live.kill()
        bystander.kill()
```

Create `src/agentos/api/conversation_routes.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""Conversation routes the W7 interface adds: background tasks (the panel), the slash command catalog and the status
line. Agents and teammates come from ``/overview``; this module covers what only the workspace knows."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.concurrency import run_in_threadpool
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

from agentos.redaction import redact_text

from .contracts import ApplicationNotFoundError, ApplicationValidationError
from .security import AuthenticatedPrincipal

MANIFEST = ".orin/logs/processes.json"
LOG_TAIL_BYTES = 4096


def _manifest(root: Path) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    path = root / MANIFEST
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}
    if isinstance(data, list):
        data = {str(item.get("pid")): item for item in data if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("pid") is not None}
    return {str(key): value for key, value in data.items() if isinstance(value, dict)} if isinstance(data, dict) else {}


def _log_tail(root: Path, relative: object) -> str:
    if not isinstance(relative, str) or not relative:
        return ""
    target = (root / relative).resolve()
    try:
        target.relative_to(root.resolve())  # a manifest edited to point outside the workspace is not read
        with target.open("rb") as handle:
            handle.seek(0, 2)
            size = handle.tell()
            handle.seek(max(0, size - LOG_TAIL_BYTES))
            return redact_text(handle.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return ""


def shell_tasks(root: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    from agentos.process_identity import same_process

    tasks = []
    for key, entry in _manifest(root).items():
        try:
            pid = int(key)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        created = entry.get("created")
        verifiable = created not in (None, "")
        running = same_process(pid, created) if verifiable else False
        tasks.append({
            "task_id": f"shell:{pid}", "kind": "shell", "pid": pid,
            "command": redact_text(str(entry.get("command") or ""))[:400],
            "description": redact_text(str(entry.get("description") or ""))[:200],
            "started_at": entry.get("started_at"), "url": entry.get("detected_url"),
            # A record written before W7 has no creation time: it is shown, never stopped from here (W6-R3).
            "state": "running" if running else ("finished" if verifiable else "unknown"),
            "stoppable": running,
            "log_tail": _log_tail(root, entry.get("log_path")),
        })
    return sorted(tasks, key=lambda item: str(item.get("started_at") or ""), reverse=True)


def stop_shell(root: Path, task_id: str) -> bool:
    from agentos.process_identity import kill_verified

    if not task_id.startswith("shell:"):
        raise ApplicationValidationError("only background shells are stopped here")
    key = task_id.split(":", 1)[1]
    entry = _manifest(root).get(key)
    if entry is None or not key.isdigit():
        raise ApplicationNotFoundError(task_id)
    return kill_verified(int(key), entry.get("created"))


def register_conversation_routes(
    app: FastAPI,
    services,
    principal_for: Callable[..., AuthenticatedPrincipal],
    conversation_root: Callable[[str, AuthenticatedPrincipal], Path],
) -> None:
    @app.get("/v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/tasks")
    async def list_tasks(conversation_id: str, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = principal_for(request)
        services.security.authorize(principal, action="conversation.read", resource_id=conversation_id, purpose="conversation.tasks")
        root = conversation_root(conversation_id, principal)
        return JSONResponse({"tasks": await run_in_threadpool(shell_tasks, root)})

    @app.post("/v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/tasks/{task_id}/stop")
    async def stop_task(conversation_id: str, task_id: str, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = principal_for(request, mutable=True)
        services.security.authorize(principal, action="conversation.send", resource_id=conversation_id, purpose="conversation.tasks")
        services.capabilities.require("shell")
        root = conversation_root(conversation_id, principal)
        stopped = await run_in_threadpool(stop_shell, root, task_id)
        return JSONResponse({"task_id": task_id, "stopped": stopped, "tasks": await run_in_threadpool(shell_tasks, root)})


__all__ = ["register_conversation_routes", "shell_tasks", "stop_shell"]
```

```python
def process_created(pid: int) -> int | str | None:
    """When the process with this pid started, read the same way for a record and for a later check (W6-R3).

    Windows: the creation time from ``GetProcessTimes``; POSIX: field 22 of ``/proc/<pid>/stat``, or where there is
    no ``/proc``, ``ps -o lstart=``. None when it cannot be read.
    """
    if pid <= 0:
        return None
    if os.name == "nt":
        from agentos.hooks.processes import _windows_creation_time, _windows_kernel32

        kernel32 = _windows_kernel32()
        handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(_PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid)
        if not handle:
            return None
        try:
            return _windows_creation_time(kernel32, handle)
        finally:
            kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
    stat = Path(f"/proc/{pid}/stat")
    if stat.is_file():
        try:
            fields = stat.read_text(encoding="utf-8").rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()
            return int(fields[19])  # starttime: field 22 counting pid and comm
        except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
            return None
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "lstart=", "-p", str(pid)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False, timeout=10)  # noqa: S603, S607
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    value = result.stdout.strip()
    return value or None


def _same_process(record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """True only when the record's pid is alive and its creation time is the one recorded at start.

    A record without a creation time (written before this check existed), or one whose time cannot be read, is
    treated as a different process: it is reported as finished and nothing is killed for it (W6-R3).
    """
    pid = int(record.get("pid") or 0)
    recorded = record.get("created")
    if pid <= 0 or recorded is None or recorded == "" or not _alive(pid):
        return False
    current = process_created(pid)
    return current is not None and current == recorded
```

with

```python
def _same_process(record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """True only when the record's pid is alive and its creation time is the one recorded at start (W6-R3)."""
    return same_process(int(record.get("pid") or 0), record.get("created"))
```

4. Replace

```python
    _cancel_turn(record)
    return _kill_verified_windows(record) if os.name == "nt" else _kill_verified_posix(record)


def _kill_verified_windows(record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Terminate the run and its descendants through handles opened and validated first; never by bare pid."""
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from agentos.hooks.processes import _close_handles, _windows_creation_time, _windows_descendants, _windows_kernel32

    kernel32 = _windows_kernel32()
    pid = int(record["pid"])
    handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(_PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | _PROCESS_TERMINATE | _SYNCHRONIZE, False, pid)
    if not handle:
        return False
    descendants: dict[int, int] = {}
    try:
        if _windows_creation_time(kernel32, handle) != record.get("created"):
            return False  # the pid now belongs to another process
        descendants = _windows_descendants(SimpleNamespace(pid=pid, _handle=handle))
        for child in descendants.values():
            kernel32.TerminateProcess(child, 1)
        return bool(kernel32.TerminateProcess(handle, 1))
    finally:
        _close_handles(descendants)
        kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)


def _kill_verified_posix(record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """SIGKILL the run's process group (it starts its own session); called only after the check."""
    import signal

    pid = int(record["pid"])
    try:
        if os.getpgid(pid) != pid:
            return False
        os.killpg(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    except OSError:
        return False
    return True
```

Create `src/agentos/process_identity.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""Which process a stored pid names, and stopping it only when it is still that process (W6-R3, W7).

A pid alone is not an identity: the OS reuses it. Records that outlive a process (``orin code --bg`` runs, background
shells) keep the creation time read here, and anything that stops one checks it again first.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

_PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 0x1000
_PROCESS_TERMINATE = 0x0001
_SYNCHRONIZE = 0x00100000


def process_created(pid: int) -> int | str | None:
    """When the process with this pid started, read the same way for a record and for a later check.

    Windows: the creation time from ``GetProcessTimes``; POSIX: field 22 of ``/proc/<pid>/stat``, or where there is
    no ``/proc``, ``ps -o lstart=``. None when it cannot be read.
    """
    if pid <= 0:
        return None
    if os.name == "nt":
        from agentos.hooks.processes import _windows_creation_time, _windows_kernel32

        kernel32 = _windows_kernel32()
        handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(_PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid)
        if not handle:
            return None
        try:
            return _windows_creation_time(kernel32, handle)
        finally:
            kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
    stat = Path(f"/proc/{pid}/stat")
    if stat.is_file():
        try:
            fields = stat.read_text(encoding="utf-8").rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()
            return int(fields[19])  # starttime: field 22 counting pid and comm
        except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
            return None
    if Path("/proc").is_dir():
        return None  # a system with /proc and no entry for this pid: the process does not exist
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "lstart=", "-p", str(pid)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False, timeout=10)  # noqa: S603, S607
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - an identity that cannot be read is None (nothing is ever stopped for it)
        return None
    value = result.stdout.strip()
    return value or None


def same_process(pid: int, created: object) -> bool:
    """True only when ``pid`` is alive and started at ``created``. A missing or unreadable time is never a match."""
    from agentos.launcher.state import process_is_alive

    if pid <= 0 or created is None or created == "" or not process_is_alive(pid):
        return False
    current = process_created(pid)
    return current is not None and current == created


def kill_verified(pid: int, created: object, *, group_only: bool = False) -> bool:
    """Stop the process (and its tree) only if it is still the one that started at ``created``.

    Windows: through a handle opened and validated first, with its validated descendants; never by bare pid.
    POSIX: its process group when it leads one (``group_only`` requires that), else the process itself.
    """
    if not same_process(pid, created):
        return False
    if os.name == "nt":
        return _kill_windows(pid, created)
    import signal

    try:
        if os.getpgid(pid) == pid:
            os.killpg(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            return True
        if group_only:
            return False
        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    except OSError:
        return False
    return True


def _kill_windows(pid: int, created: object) -> bool:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from agentos.hooks.processes import _close_handles, _windows_creation_time, _windows_descendants, _windows_kernel32

    kernel32 = _windows_kernel32()
    handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(_PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | _PROCESS_TERMINATE | _SYNCHRONIZE, False, pid)
    if not handle:
        return False
    descendants: dict[int, int] = {}
    try:
        if _windows_creation_time(kernel32, handle) != created:
            return False  # the pid now belongs to another process
        descendants = _windows_descendants(SimpleNamespace(pid=pid, _handle=handle))
        for child in descendants.values():
            kernel32.TerminateProcess(child, 1)
        return bool(kernel32.TerminateProcess(handle, 1))
    finally:
        _close_handles(descendants)
        kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)


__all__ = ["kill_verified", "process_created", "same_process"]
```

```python
"""W7: the status line runs the person's command with the session as JSON (trusted folders only), and the command
catalog lists the folder's skills and commands."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.configuration.mode import RuntimeMode
from agentos.settings.paths import orin_user_dir

from tests.unit.api.test_w7_settings_api import AUTH, _client, _write

SCRIPT = "import sys, json; d = json.load(sys.stdin); print('model=' + d['model']['id']); print('dir=' + d['workspace']['current_dir'])"


def _statusline(client):
    return client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/statusline", headers={**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "s"}).json()


def test_the_status_line_waits_for_trust_then_prints_the_commands_lines(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    script = tmp_path / "line.py"
    script.write_text(SCRIPT, encoding="utf-8")
    _write(orin_user_dir() / "settings.json", {"statusLine": {"type": "command", "command": f'"{sys.executable}" "{script}"'}})
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    assert _statusline(client) == {"enabled": False, "lines": [], "error": "workspace_not_trusted"}
    client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers={**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "t"})
    answer = _statusline(client)
    assert answer["enabled"] is True and answer["error"] is None
    assert answer["lines"][0] == "model=m" and answer["lines"][1].startswith("dir=")


def test_no_status_line_without_a_setting_or_with_hooks_disabled(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers={**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "t"})
    assert _statusline(client)["error"] == "not_configured"
    _write(orin_user_dir() / "settings.json", {"statusLine": {"type": "command", "command": "echo hi"}, "disableAllHooks": True})
    assert _statusline(client)["error"] == "hooks_disabled"


def test_a_failing_command_reports_it(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    _write(orin_user_dir() / "settings.json", {"statusLine": {"type": "command", "command": f'"{sys.executable}" -c "import sys; sys.exit(3)"'}})
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    client.post("/v1/conversations/c1/workspace-trust", headers={**AUTH, "Idempotency-Key": "t"})
    assert _statusline(client) == {"enabled": True, "lines": [], "error": "command_failed"}


def test_a_shared_server_runs_no_status_line(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path, RuntimeMode.SERVER)
    assert _statusline(client)["error"] == "shell_unavailable"


def test_the_catalog_lists_the_skills_a_person_can_run(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    for name, extra in (("review-pr", ""), ("internal", "user-invocable: false\n")):
        folder = orin_user_dir() / "skills" / name
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (folder / "SKILL.md").write_text(f"---\ndescription: Does {name}\nargument-hint: <pr>\n{extra}---\nBody", encoding="utf-8")
    body = _client(tmp_path).get("/v1/conversations/c1/commands", headers=AUTH).json()
    names = [item["name"] for item in body["commands"]]
    assert "review-pr" in names and "internal" not in names
    item = next(entry for entry in body["commands"] if entry["name"] == "review-pr")
    assert item["description"] == "Does review-pr" and item["argument_hint"] == "<pr>" and item["origin"] == "user"
    assert json.dumps(body)  # plain JSON
```

```python

STATUSLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 5.0
STATUSLINE_MAX_LINES = 5
STATUSLINE_MAX_CHARS = 400
STATUSLINE_CACHE_SECONDS = 1.0
_STATUSLINE_CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, str, dict[str, Any]]] = {}
_STATUSLINE_LOCKS: dict[str, Any] = {}


def statusline_command(settings) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
    """``(command, None)`` when the status line may run, else ``(None, why not)``. It runs only from a trusted folder
    (the caller checks), never while hooks are disabled, and only from managed policy when that is all it allows."""
    raw = settings.get("statusLine")
    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or raw.get("type") != "command" or not str(raw.get("command") or "").strip():
        return None, "not_configured"
    if settings.get("disableAllHooks") is True:
        return None, "hooks_disabled"
    if settings.get("allowManagedHooksOnly") is True and settings.provenance.get("statusLine") != "managed":
        return None, "managed_hooks_only"
    return str(raw["command"]), None


def statusline_input(conversation: dict[str, Any], root: Path, settings, *, added_dirs: list[str], usage: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """What the status line command reads on stdin: the documented fields Orin knows (others are omitted)."""
    from agentos.version import __version__

    model = str(conversation.get("model_id") or "")
    style = settings.get("outputStyle")
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "session_id": str(conversation.get("conversation_id") or ""),
        "session_name": str(conversation.get("title") or "") or None,
        "cwd": str(root),
        "model": {"id": model, "display_name": model.rsplit("/", 1)[-1] or model},
        "workspace": {"current_dir": str(root), "project_dir": str(root), "added_dirs": added_dirs},
        "version": __version__,
        "output_style": {"name": style if isinstance(style, str) and style else "default"},
    }
    cost = usage.get("estimated_cost_usd")
    if isinstance(cost, (int, float)):
        payload["cost"] = {"total_cost_usd": float(cost)}
    tokens_in, tokens_out = usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens")
    if isinstance(tokens_in, int) and isinstance(tokens_out, int):
        payload["context_window"] = {"total_input_tokens": tokens_in, "total_output_tokens": tokens_out}
    return payload


def run_statusline(conversation_id: str, command: str, root: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run the command with the JSON on stdin; its first lines are the status line (ANSI colours kept)."""
    import os
    import threading
    import time

    from agentos.hooks.processes import run_shell_command

    digest = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True) + "\x00" + command
    lock = _STATUSLINE_LOCKS.setdefault(conversation_id, threading.Lock())
    with lock:  # one run per conversation at a time; a repeat within a second reuses the last answer
        cached = _STATUSLINE_CACHE.get(conversation_id)
        if cached is not None and cached[1] == digest and time.monotonic() - cached[0] < STATUSLINE_CACHE_SECONDS:
            return cached[2]
        environment = {**os.environ, "COLUMNS": "120", "LINES": "1"}
        result = run_shell_command(command, cwd=str(root), env=environment, stdin_text=json.dumps(payload), timeout_seconds=STATUSLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
        if result.timed_out or result.error or result.exit_code not in (0, None):
            answer = {"enabled": True, "lines": [], "error": "timed_out" if result.timed_out else "command_failed"}
        else:
            lines = [line[:STATUSLINE_MAX_CHARS] for line in result.stdout.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n") if line.strip()]
            answer = {"enabled": True, "lines": lines[:STATUSLINE_MAX_LINES], "error": None}
        _STATUSLINE_CACHE[conversation_id] = (time.monotonic(), digest, answer)
        return answer


def command_catalog(root: Path | None, trusted: bool, *, personal: bool) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The skills and commands a person can run with ``/name`` in this folder (built-ins live in the interface)."""
    from agentos.skills.disk import load_disk_skills

    catalog = load_disk_skills(workspace_root=root, trusted=trusted, personal=personal)
    return sorted(
        ({"name": skill.name, "description": " ".join(skill.description.split())[:300], "kind": skill.kind,
          "origin": skill.origin, "argument_hint": skill.argument_hint[:120], "context": skill.context}
         for skill in catalog.skills.values() if skill.user_invocable),
        key=lambda item: item["name"],
    )
```
