```python
    @app.get("/v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/commands")
    async def list_commands(conversation_id: str, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        principal = principal_for(request)
        services.security.authorize(principal, action="conversation.read", resource_id=conversation_id, purpose="conversation.commands")
        root = conversation_root(conversation_id, principal)
        is_trusted = await run_in_threadpool(trusted, principal, root)
        items = await run_in_threadpool(lambda: command_catalog(root, is_trusted, personal=services.capabilities.host_folders))
        return JSONResponse({"commands": items, "trusted": is_trusted})

    @app.post("/v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/statusline")
    async def statusline(conversation_id: str, request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        """The status line (settings ``statusLine``): a command that reads the session as JSON and prints a line."""
        from agentos.settings import load_effective_settings

        principal = principal_for(request, mutable=True)
        services.security.authorize(principal, action="conversation.read", resource_id=conversation_id, purpose="conversation.statusline")
        root = conversation_root(conversation_id, principal)  # the conversation must be this person's, whatever else
        if not services.capabilities.shell:
            return JSONResponse({"enabled": False, "lines": [], "error": "shell_unavailable"})
        is_trusted = await run_in_threadpool(trusted, principal, root)
        if not is_trusted:  # as in Claude Code: the line stays blank until the folder is trusted
            return JSONResponse({"enabled": False, "lines": [], "error": "workspace_not_trusted"})
        settings = await run_in_threadpool(lambda: load_effective_settings(root, trusted=True))
        command, why_not = statusline_command(settings)
        if command is None:
            return JSONResponse({"enabled": False, "lines": [], "error": why_not})
        application = services.conversation_application
        conversation = await run_in_threadpool(application.get, conversation_id, principal.user_id) if application is not None else {}
        usage: dict[str, Any] = {}
        try:
            overview = await run_in_threadpool(application.overview, conversation_id, principal.user_id)
            usage = dict((overview or {}).get("token_usage") or {}) if isinstance(overview, dict) else {}
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the line runs without cost figures rather than not at all
            usage = {}
        added: list[str] = []
        directories = services.conversation_directories
        if directories is not None and services.capabilities.host_folders:
            added = [str(path) for path in await run_in_threadpool(directories.paths, principal.user_id, conversation_id)]
        payload = statusline_input(dict(conversation or {}), root, settings, added_dirs=added, usage=usage)
        return JSONResponse(await run_in_threadpool(run_statusline, conversation_id, command, root, payload))
```

### Task 5: Hook events UserPromptExpansion and Notification

UserPromptExpansion fires before a typed `/command` (a disk skill or command, or an MCP prompt) expands, with `expansion_type`, `command_name`, `command_args`, `command_source` and `prompt`, matched on `command_name`; a hook may block it (the model is told why) or add context (JSON `additionalContext` or plain stdout). A permission prompt fires Notification with `notification_type: permission_prompt`. (W0 ledger; idle notifications and MessageDisplay are not implemented — decision 4.)

- Create: `tests/unit/hooks/test_w7_expansion_and_notification.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/session.py`, `src/agentos/hooks/matching.py`, `src/agentos/hooks/protocol.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `TurnSession._prompt_expansion_allowed(...)`, `._notify_permission_prompt(summary)`; `MATCH_FIELDS[USER_PROMPT_EXPANSION] = "command_name"`.

```python
"""W7: UserPromptExpansion sees a typed command before it expands (and may block it); a permission prompt fires a
Notification hook with notification_type permission_prompt."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.lifecycle import HookEvent, LifecycleBus, LifecycleDecision
from agentos.hooks.matching import matcher_applies
from agentos.settings.paths import orin_user_dir

from agentos.agentic.session import TurnSession

from tests.unit.cli_code.test_cli_turn_session import _Agents, _Store
from tests.unit.hooks.test_session_hooks_w2 import _user


def _skill(name: str) -> None:
    folder = orin_user_dir() / "skills" / name
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (folder / "SKILL.md").write_text(f"---\ndescription: {name}\n---\nSKILL-BODY-{name}", encoding="utf-8")


def _session(tmp_path: Path, prompt: str) -> TurnSession:
    store = _Store()
    store.history_for_turn = lambda turn: [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    session = TurnSession(turn={**store.turn, "code_mode": "code"}, store=store, agents_store=_Agents(), memory_store=None,
                          provider_factory=lambda: object(), workspace_root=tmp_path, enable_subagents=False)
    session.plugin_hooks = True  # the local profile: the person's own skills load
    return session


def _texts(session: TurnSession) -> str:
    return "\n".join(item.text for item in session._reminders._items)


def test_expansion_matches_on_the_command_name() -> None:
    assert matcher_applies(HookEvent.USER_PROMPT_EXPANSION, "deploy", {"command_name": "deploy"})
    assert not matcher_applies(HookEvent.USER_PROMPT_EXPANSION, "deploy", {"command_name": "review"})


def test_a_hook_blocks_a_typed_command_and_another_adds_context(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    _skill("deploy")
    _skill("review")
    block = tmp_path / "block.py"
    block.write_text("import sys; print('deploys need a ticket', file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(2)", encoding="utf-8")
    note = tmp_path / "note.py"
    note.write_text("import json, sys; d = json.load(sys.stdin); print('expanded ' + d['command_name'] + ' via ' + d['expansion_type'])", encoding="utf-8")
    _user({"hooks": {"UserPromptExpansion": [
        {"matcher": "deploy", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": f'"{sys.executable}" "{block}"'}]},
        {"matcher": "review", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": f'"{sys.executable}" "{note}"'}]},
    ]}})
    blocked = _session(tmp_path / "a", "/deploy now")
    blocked.build_runtime()
    reminders = _texts(blocked)
    assert "SKILL-BODY-deploy" not in reminders and "deploys need a ticket" in reminders
    allowed = _session(tmp_path / "b", "/review 12")
    allowed.build_runtime()
    text = _texts(allowed)
    assert "SKILL-BODY-review" in text and "expanded review via slash_command" in text


def test_a_permission_prompt_notifies() -> None:
    events: list = []
    bus = LifecycleBus()
    bus.subscribe(lambda event: events.append(event) or LifecycleDecision())

    class _Session:
        lifecycle = bus

    TurnSession._notify_permission_prompt(_Session(), "Permissão necessária para executar `rm x`")  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert [(event.name, event.payload["notification_type"]) for event in events] == [(HookEvent.NOTIFICATION, "permission_prompt")]
    assert "rm x" in events[0].payload["message"]
```

```python
            return
        try:
            bus.emit(HookEvent.NOTIFICATION, message=str(summary)[:500], title="Orin", notification_type="permission_prompt")
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a notification never stops a turn
            logger.exception("Notification hooks failed")

    def _prompt_expansion_allowed(self, expansion_type: str, name: str, arguments: str, source: str, prompt: str) -> bool:
        """UserPromptExpansion (W7): hooks see a typed command before it expands; one may block it or add context."""
        from agentos.agentic.reminders import COMMAND_KIND

        bus = self.lifecycle
        if bus is None:
            return True
        try:
            decision = bus.emit(
                HookEvent.USER_PROMPT_EXPANSION, expansion_type=expansion_type, command_name=name,
                command_args=arguments, command_source=source, prompt=prompt,
            )
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - lifecycle never stops a turn
            logger.exception("UserPromptExpansion hooks failed")
            return True
        for index, context in enumerate(decision.additional_context):
            self._reminders.add(COMMAND_KIND, context, key=f"expansion:{name}:{index}")
        if decision.blocked:
            reason = decision.reason or "blocked by a hook"
            self._reminders.add(COMMAND_KIND, f"/{name} did not run: a hook blocked it ({reason[:500]}). Tell the person.", key=f"command:{name}")
            return False
        return True

    def _apply_mcp_prompt(self, name: str, arguments: str) -> None:
```

### Task 6: CLI transcripts expire everywhere; the CLI's data folders are owner-only (W6-R9, W6-R15)

W6-R9: writing a transcript entry also sweeps every conversation's folder (at most once an hour per process), removing entries past the 7-day retention and folders left empty. W6-R15: `make_private_dir` creates a folder owner-only — on Windows a protected access list for the current account inherited by what is created inside, on POSIX mode 0700 — and the transcripts and host requests are written only inside such a folder (fail closed). The session index is not restricted (decision 6).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/cli_code/test_w7_cli_data_privacy.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/cli_transcript.py`, `src/agentos/agentic/host_requests.py`, `src/agentos/installation/api_token.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `api_token.make_private_dir(path)`, `_windows_owner_only(path, *, inherit=False)`; `cli_transcript.sweep_expired(root, *, clock, retention)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (test files and their fixtures).

Create `tests/unit/cli_code/test_w7_cli_data_privacy.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""W7 (W6-R9, W6-R15): CLI transcripts expire in every conversation's folder, and the CLI's data folders are owner-only."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from agentos.agentic import cli_transcript
from agentos.agentic.cli_transcript import RETENTION_SECONDS, TranscriptStore, sweep_expired
from agentos.installation.api_token import make_private_dir


def test_an_old_entry_of_another_conversation_is_removed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    old = TranscriptStore("finished", root=tmp_path)
    entry = old.write({"tool_result": "a secret file"})
    stale = old.folder / f"{entry}.json"
    past = time.time() - RETENTION_SECONDS - 3600
    os.utime(stale, (past, past))
    kept = TranscriptStore("finished-recently", root=tmp_path)
    recent = kept.write({"tool_result": "x"})
    cli_transcript._LAST_SWEEP.clear()
    TranscriptStore("active", root=tmp_path).write({"tool_result": "y"})
    assert not stale.exists() and not old.folder.exists()  # the emptied folder goes too
    assert (kept.folder / f"{recent}.json").exists()


def test_the_sweep_runs_at_most_once_an_hour(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = tmp_path / "conv"
    folder.mkdir()
    stale = folder / "tr_x.json"
    stale.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    past = time.time() - RETENTION_SECONDS - 10
    os.utime(stale, (past, past))
    cli_transcript._LAST_SWEEP.clear()
    sweep_expired(tmp_path, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0 + time.time())  # a first sweep from a clock far ahead
    assert not stale.exists()
    stale.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    stale.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    os.utime(stale, (past, past))
    sweep_expired(tmp_path, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0 + time.time() + 60)  # a minute later: skipped
    assert stale.exists()


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name != "nt", reason="Windows ACLs")
def test_a_private_folder_and_its_new_files_are_owner_only_on_windows(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = make_private_dir(tmp_path / "cli-transcripts")
    (folder / "entry.json").write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    for target in (folder, folder / "entry.json"):
        listing = subprocess.run(["icacls", str(target)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
        entries = [line.strip() for line in listing.splitlines()[:-1] if ":(" in line]
        assert len(entries) == 1 and os.environ["USERNAME"].lower() in entries[0].lower(), listing


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name == "nt", reason="POSIX modes")
def test_a_private_folder_is_mode_700_on_posix(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = make_private_dir(tmp_path / "cli-transcripts")
    assert (folder.stat().st_mode & 0o777) == 0o700
```

```python

def sweep_expired(root: Path, *, clock=time.time, retention: float = RETENTION_SECONDS) -> None:
    """W6-R9: entries past the retention are removed from every conversation's folder, not only the one being written
    to (a finished conversation's tool results do not stay forever); empty folders go too. At most once an hour."""
    now = clock()
    key = str(root)
    if now - _LAST_SWEEP.get(key, float("-inf")) < SWEEP_EVERY_SECONDS:
        return
    _LAST_SWEEP[key] = now
    cutoff = now - retention
    try:
        folders = [item for item in root.iterdir() if item.is_dir() and not item.is_symlink()]
    except OSError:
        return
    for folder in folders:
        try:
            for item in folder.iterdir():
                try:
                    if item.is_file() and item.stat().st_mtime < cutoff:
                        item.unlink()
                except OSError:
                    continue
            if not any(folder.iterdir()):
                folder.rmdir()
        except OSError:
            continue


__all__ = ["MAX_ENTRY_BYTES", "RETENTION_SECONDS", "TranscriptStore", "sweep_expired", "transcripts_root"]
```

```python
        os.chmod(path, 0o600)


_PRIVATE_DIRS: set[str] = set()


def make_private_dir(path: Path) -> Path:
    """Create ``path`` (and its parents) and make it owner-only, its new files included (W6-R15). Raises ``OSError``
    when the folder cannot be restricted: the caller then keeps nothing in it (fail closed)."""
    target = Path(path)
    target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    key = os.path.normcase(str(target.resolve()))
    if key in _PRIVATE_DIRS:
        return target
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            _windows_owner_only(target, inherit=True)
        else:
            os.chmod(target, 0o700)
    except ApiTokenError as error:
        raise OSError(str(error)) from error
    _PRIVATE_DIRS.add(key)
    return target
```

## Batch B — Frontend foundations (Tasks 7–11)

**Every "Create" below is a new file: if a file of that name exists, stop and report — never overwrite it.**

### Decisions recorded while planning Batch B (the ledger repeats them)

7. **A built-in command is offered only once the chat implements it**; one typed before its surface exists (Batches C–D) is sent as an ordinary message, as today.
8. **`/ultraplan` is left out** (Claude Code removed it; plan mode replaces it).
9. **Keybindings:** Orin implements a subset of the documented actions; a binding to a documented action Orin lacks is kept in the file and reported as a warning; a browser has no default for keys it keeps for itself (`ctrl+t/w/n/l/tab`), the desktop app does.
10. **The `!` shell mode and `@server:uri` MCP resources move to Batch C** (both need backend routes).
11. **Picking a built-in without arguments runs it at once** (Claude Code's behaviour); one with arguments fills the input.
12. **Releases bump `frontend/package.json` and its lockfile** from now on (spec §10: the frontend's version is aligned).

### Task 7: Frontend dependencies pinned and the frontend version aligned

Every `latest` in `frontend/package.json` becomes the exact version the lockfile already resolves (nothing is upgraded: only the lockfile's root entries change), and the frontend's version follows the release (0.9.4). `test_versions.py` checks the frontend's version and that no dependency floats (`latest`, `next`, `*`, empty). From now on the release step bumps `frontend/package.json` and `frontend/package-lock.json` (2 places) too.

```python
def test_frontend_dependencies_are_pinned() -> None:
    """W7: no dependency floats to whatever is newest on the day of an install."""

    assert floating == {}
```

### Task 8: API client for the W7 routes; an API error keeps the server's problems

`ApiError.problems` holds a 422's `details.problems` (at most 50 strings of 500 characters; never in `toPublicDetails`). `api/parse.ts` has small readers for untrusted JSON. `api/settings.ts` covers Batch A's settings routes; `api/sessionTools.ts` the conversation tools: queue, compact, context, file history and per-message code rewind, background tasks, the command catalog, the status line, `/v1/instance` and `/add-dir`. Every parser drops malformed entries instead of trusting them.

**Files:**

```python
/** W7: what a 422 names as wrong (`details.problems`), bounded so a hostile server cannot flood the interface. */

const MAX_PROBLEM_CHARS = 500
```

```python
    problems: isRecord(envelope.details) ? envelope.details.problems : undefined,
  })
}

function safeProblems(value: unknown): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return []
  return value
    .filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string' && item.trim().length > 0)
    .slice(0, MAX_PROBLEMS)
    .map((item) => item.trim().slice(0, MAX_PROBLEM_CHARS))
}
```

Create `frontend/src/api/parse.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/** Small readers for untrusted JSON (W7 API modules): every value is checked, nothing is cast blindly. */

export function rec(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
}

export function str(value: unknown, fallback = ''): string {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : fallback
}

export function optStr(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 ? value : null
}

export function num(value: unknown, fallback = 0): number {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback
}

export function bool(value: unknown): boolean {
  return value === true
}

export function list<T>(value: unknown, read: (item: unknown) => T | null, max = 1000): T[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return []
  const items: T[] = []
  for (const item of value.slice(0, max)) {
    const parsed = read(item)
    if (parsed !== null) items.push(parsed)
  }
  return items
}

export function strings(value: unknown, max = 1000): string[] {
  return list(value, (item) => (typeof item === 'string' ? item : null), max)
}
```

Create `frontend/src/api/sessionTools.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * A Code session's tools beyond sending a message (W7): the message queue, /compact, /context, the file history and
 * per-message rewind, background tasks, the command catalog and the status line.
 */
import type { ApiClient } from './client'
import { bool, list, num, optStr, rec, str, strings } from './parse'

const base = (conversationId: string) => `/v1/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(conversationId)}`

export type QueuedInput = { queueId: string; content: string; createdAt: string | null }

function queued(value: unknown): QueuedInput | null {
  const item = rec(value)
  return str(item.queue_id) ? { queueId: str(item.queue_id), content: str(item.content), createdAt: optStr(item.created_at) } : null
}

export function listQueue(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string): Promise<QueuedInput[]> {
  return client.request({ path: `${base(conversationId)}/queue`, parse: (value) => list(rec(value).items, queued) })
}

/** Sends now when the conversation is idle, or queues the text for the running turn. */
export function enqueueMessage(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, message: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()):
  Promise<{ queued: boolean; queueId: string | null; turnId: string | null }> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/queue`, method: 'POST', intent, body: { message },
    parse: (value) => {
      const data = rec(value)
      return { queued: bool(data.queued), queueId: optStr(data.queue_id), turnId: optStr(data.turn_id) }
    },
  })
}

export function removeQueued(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, queueId: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()): Promise<boolean> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/queue/${encodeURIComponent(queueId)}`, method: 'DELETE', intent,
    parse: (value) => bool(rec(value).cancelled),
  })
}

export function compactConversation(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, instructions?: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()):
  Promise<{ turnId: string | null }> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/compact`, method: 'POST', intent,
    body: instructions?.trim() ? { instructions: instructions.trim() } : {},
    parse: (value) => ({ turnId: optStr(rec(value).turn_id) }),
  })
}

export type ContextCategory = { key: string; tokens: number }
export type ContextReport = {
  available: boolean
  limitTokens: number
  usedTokens: number
  percentage: number
  compactionCount: number
  categories: ContextCategory[]
  measuredAt: string | null
}

export function parseContextReport(value: unknown): ContextReport {
  const data = rec(value)
  return {
    available: bool(data.available), limitTokens: num(data.limit_tokens), usedTokens: num(data.used_tokens),
    percentage: num(data.percentage), compactionCount: num(data.compaction_count),
    categories: list(data.categories, (item) => { const category = rec(item); return str(category.key) ? { key: str(category.key), tokens: Math.max(0, num(category.tokens)) } : null }),
    measuredAt: optStr(data.measured_at),
  }
}

export function getContext(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string): Promise<ContextReport> {
  return client.request({ path: `${base(conversationId)}/context`, parse: parseContextReport })
}

export type FileHistoryItem = { messageId: string; turnId: string; kind: string; createdAt: string | null; rewoundAt: string | null; files: string[] }

export function getFileHistory(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string): Promise<FileHistoryItem[]> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/file-history`,
    parse: (value) => list(rec(value).items, (item) => {
      const entry = rec(item)
      return str(entry.message_id) ? {
        messageId: str(entry.message_id), turnId: str(entry.turn_id), kind: str(entry.kind, 'message'),
        createdAt: optStr(entry.created_at), rewoundAt: optStr(entry.rewound_at), files: strings(entry.files),
      } : null
    }),
  })
}

export type CodeRewindResult = {
  messageId: string
  restored: string[]
  deleted: string[]
  unchanged: string[]
  skipped: Array<{ path: string; reason: string }>
  blocked: boolean
  undoId: string | null
}

export function rewindCode(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, messageId: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()): Promise<CodeRewindResult> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/messages/${encodeURIComponent(messageId)}/rewind`, method: 'POST', intent, body: { mode: 'code' },
    parse: (value) => {
      const data = rec(value)
      return {
        messageId: str(data.message_id, messageId), restored: strings(data.restored), deleted: strings(data.deleted),
        unchanged: strings(data.unchanged),
        skipped: list(data.skipped, (item) => { const entry = rec(item); return { path: str(entry.path), reason: str(entry.reason) } }),
        blocked: bool(data.blocked), undoId: optStr(data.undo_id),
      }
    },
  })
}

export type ShellTask = {
  taskId: string
  kind: 'shell'
  pid: number
  command: string
  description: string
  startedAt: string | null
  url: string | null
  state: 'running' | 'finished' | 'unknown'
  stoppable: boolean
  logTail: string
}

function shellTask(value: unknown): ShellTask | null {
  const item = rec(value)
  if (!str(item.task_id) || item.kind !== 'shell') return null
  const state = item.state === 'running' || item.state === 'finished' ? item.state : 'unknown'
  return {
    taskId: str(item.task_id), kind: 'shell', pid: num(item.pid), command: str(item.command), description: str(item.description),
    startedAt: optStr(item.started_at), url: optStr(item.url), state, stoppable: bool(item.stoppable), logTail: str(item.log_tail),
  }
}

export function listTasks(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string): Promise<ShellTask[]> {
  return client.request({ path: `${base(conversationId)}/tasks`, parse: (value) => list(rec(value).tasks, shellTask) })
}

export function stopTask(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, taskId: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()):
  Promise<{ stopped: boolean; tasks: ShellTask[] }> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(taskId)}/stop`, method: 'POST', intent, body: {},
    parse: (value) => { const data = rec(value); return { stopped: bool(data.stopped), tasks: list(data.tasks, shellTask) } },
  })
}

export type CatalogCommand = { name: string; description: string; kind: string; origin: string; argumentHint: string; context: string | null }

export function listCommandCatalog(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string): Promise<{ commands: CatalogCommand[]; trusted: boolean }> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/commands`,
    parse: (value) => {
      const data = rec(value)
      return {
        trusted: bool(data.trusted),
        commands: list(data.commands, (item) => {
          const command = rec(item)
          return str(command.name) ? {
            name: str(command.name), description: str(command.description), kind: str(command.kind), origin: str(command.origin),
            argumentHint: str(command.argument_hint), context: optStr(command.context),
          } : null
        }, 500),
      }
    },
  })
}

export type StatusLine = { enabled: boolean; lines: string[]; error: string | null }

export function runStatusLine(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, signal?: AbortSignal, intent = client.createMutationIntent()): Promise<StatusLine> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/statusline`, method: 'POST', intent, body: {}, signal,
    parse: (value) => {
      const data = rec(value)
      return { enabled: bool(data.enabled), lines: strings(data.lines, 5), error: optStr(data.error) }
    },
  })
}

export type InstanceInfo = { version: string; mode: string; capabilities: Record<string, boolean> }

export function getInstance(client: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<InstanceInfo> {
  return client.request({
    path: '/v1/instance', signal,
    parse: (value) => {
      const data = rec(value)
      const capabilities = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(rec(data.capabilities)).filter((entry): entry is [string, boolean] => typeof entry[1] === 'boolean'))
      return { version: str(data.version), mode: str(data.mode), capabilities }
    },
  })
}

export type AddedDirectory = { directoryId: string; path: string }

/** `/add-dir`: a folder this session may also read and write (local profile only). */
export function addDirectory(client: ApiClient, conversationId: string, path: string, intent = client.createMutationIntent()): Promise<AddedDirectory> {
  return client.request({
    path: `${base(conversationId)}/directories`, method: 'POST', intent, body: { path },
    parse: (value) => { const data = rec(value); return { directoryId: str(data.directory_id), path: str(data.path, path) } },
  })
}
```

Create `frontend/src/api/settings.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * The settings the interface edits (W7): effective settings with provenance, permission rules per layer, hooks,
 * agents, output styles, keybindings and trusted folders. Local profile only: a shared server answers 404.
 */
import type { ApiClient } from './client'
import { bool, list, num, optStr, rec, str, strings } from './parse'

export type SettingsLayerName = 'user' | 'project' | 'local' | 'cli' | 'managed'
export type WritableLayer = 'user' | 'project' | 'local'
export type RuleKind = 'allow' | 'ask' | 'deny' | 'additionalDirectories'

export type RuleEntry = { rule: string; layer: SettingsLayerName; file: string | null; duplicate: boolean }
export type SettingsFile = { path: string; exists: boolean }

export type EffectiveSettings = {
  projectRoot: string | null
  trusted: boolean
  values: Record<string, unknown>
  provenance: Record<string, string>
  layers: Array<{ name: SettingsLayerName; file: string | null; data: Record<string, unknown> }>
  files: Record<string, SettingsFile[]>
  writable: Record<WritableLayer, string | null>
  permissions: Record<RuleKind, RuleEntry[]>
  errors: Array<{ file: string; message: string }>
  untrustedIgnored: string[]
}

const LAYERS: readonly SettingsLayerName[] = ['user', 'project', 'local', 'cli', 'managed']
const KINDS: readonly RuleKind[] = ['allow', 'ask', 'deny', 'additionalDirectories']

function layerName(value: unknown): SettingsLayerName | null {
  return LAYERS.includes(value as SettingsLayerName) ? (value as SettingsLayerName) : null
}

function ruleEntry(value: unknown): RuleEntry | null {
  const data = rec(value)
  const layer = layerName(data.layer)
  const rule = str(data.rule)
  return layer && rule ? { rule, layer, file: optStr(data.file), duplicate: bool(data.duplicate) } : null
}

function permissionsOf(value: unknown): Record<RuleKind, RuleEntry[]> {
  const data = rec(value)
  return Object.fromEntries(KINDS.map((kind) => [kind, list(data[kind], ruleEntry)])) as Record<RuleKind, RuleEntry[]>
}

function stringMap(value: unknown): Record<string, string> {
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(rec(value)).filter((entry): entry is [string, string] => typeof entry[1] === 'string'))
}

export function parseEffectiveSettings(value: unknown): EffectiveSettings {
  const data = rec(value)
  const writable = rec(data.writable)
  return {
    projectRoot: optStr(data.project_root),
    trusted: bool(data.trusted),
    values: rec(data.values),
    provenance: stringMap(data.provenance),
    layers: list(data.layers, (item) => {
      const layer = rec(item)
      const name = layerName(layer.name)
      return name ? { name, file: optStr(layer.file), data: rec(layer.data) } : null
    }),
    files: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(rec(data.files)).map(([layer, items]) => [
      layer, list(items, (item) => { const file = rec(item); return str(file.path) ? { path: str(file.path), exists: bool(file.exists) } : null }),
    ])),
    writable: { user: optStr(writable.user), project: optStr(writable.project), local: optStr(writable.local) },
    permissions: permissionsOf(data.permissions),
    errors: list(data.errors, (item) => { const error = rec(item); return { file: str(error.file), message: str(error.message) } }),
    untrustedIgnored: strings(data.untrusted_ignored),
  }
}

function query(conversationId?: string | null) {
  return conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : undefined
}

export function getEffectiveSettings(client: ApiClient, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<EffectiveSettings> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/effective', query: query(conversationId), parse: parseEffectiveSettings })
}

export type RuleChange = { layer: WritableLayer; kind: RuleKind; rule: string; op: 'add' | 'remove'; conversationId?: string | null }

export function changePermissionRule(client: ApiClient, change: RuleChange): Promise<Record<RuleKind, RuleEntry[]>> {
  return client.request({
    path: '/v1/settings/permissions',
    method: 'PUT',
    body: { layer: change.layer, kind: change.kind, rule: change.rule, op: change.op, ...(change.conversationId ? { conversation_id: change.conversationId } : {}) },
    parse: (value) => permissionsOf(rec(value).permissions),
  })
}

export type HookSpec = { event: string; matcher: string; type: string; source: string; label: string; timeout: number }
export type HooksReport = {
  disabled: boolean
  effective: HookSpec[]
  editable: Partial<Record<WritableLayer, { file: string; hooks: Record<string, unknown> }>>
  errors: Array<{ source: string; message: string }>
}

export function parseHooksReport(value: unknown): HooksReport {
  const data = rec(value)
  const editable: HooksReport['editable'] = {}
  for (const [layer, entry] of Object.entries(rec(data.editable))) {
    if (layer === 'user' || layer === 'project' || layer === 'local') {
      const item = rec(entry)
      editable[layer] = { file: str(item.file), hooks: rec(item.hooks) }
    }
  }
  return {
    disabled: bool(data.disabled),
    effective: list(data.effective, (item) => {
      const spec = rec(item)
      return { event: str(spec.event), matcher: str(spec.matcher), type: str(spec.type), source: str(spec.source), label: str(spec.label), timeout: num(spec.timeout) }
    }),
    editable,
    errors: list(data.errors, (item) => { const error = rec(item); return { source: str(error.source), message: str(error.message) } }),
  }
}

export function getHooks(client: ApiClient, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<HooksReport> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/hooks', query: query(conversationId), parse: parseHooksReport })
}

export function saveHooks(client: ApiClient, layer: WritableLayer, hooks: Record<string, unknown>, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<HooksReport> {
  return client.request({
    path: '/v1/settings/hooks', method: 'PUT',
    body: { layer, hooks, ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}) },
    parse: parseHooksReport,
  })
}

export type AgentDefinition = {
  name: string
  description: string
  origin: string
  path: string | null
  editable: boolean
  prompt: string
  tools: string[] | null
  disallowedTools: string[]
  model: string | null
  permissionMode: string | null
  maxTurns: number | null
  effort: string | null
}
export type AgentsReport = {
  agents: AgentDefinition[]
  errors: Array<{ file: string; message: string }>
  shadowed: Array<{ name: string; kept: string; ignored: string }>
}

export function parseAgentsReport(value: unknown): AgentsReport {
  const data = rec(value)
  return {
    agents: list(data.agents, (item) => {
      const agent = rec(item)
      if (!str(agent.name)) return null
      return {
        name: str(agent.name), description: str(agent.description), origin: str(agent.origin), path: optStr(agent.path),
        editable: bool(agent.editable), prompt: str(agent.prompt),
        tools: Array.isArray(agent.tools) ? strings(agent.tools) : null, disallowedTools: strings(agent.disallowed_tools),
        model: optStr(agent.model), permissionMode: optStr(agent.permission_mode),
        maxTurns: typeof agent.max_turns === 'number' ? agent.max_turns : null, effort: optStr(agent.effort),
      }
    }, 200),
    errors: list(data.errors, (item) => { const error = rec(item); return { file: str(error.file), message: str(error.message) } }),
    shadowed: list(data.shadowed, (item) => { const entry = rec(item); return { name: str(entry.name), kept: str(entry.kept), ignored: str(entry.ignored) } }),
  }
}

export function getAgents(client: ApiClient, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<AgentsReport> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/agents', query: query(conversationId), parse: parseAgentsReport })
}

export type AgentDraft = {
  scope: 'user' | 'project'
  description: string
  prompt: string
  tools: string[] | null
  disallowedTools: string[]
  model: string | null
  permissionMode: string | null
  maxTurns: number | null
  effort: string | null
}

export function saveAgent(client: ApiClient, name: string, draft: AgentDraft, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<AgentsReport> {
  return client.request({
    path: `/v1/settings/agents/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`,
    method: 'PUT',
    body: {
      scope: draft.scope, description: draft.description, prompt: draft.prompt, tools: draft.tools,
      disallowed_tools: draft.disallowedTools, ...(draft.model ? { model: draft.model } : {}),
      ...(draft.permissionMode ? { permission_mode: draft.permissionMode } : {}),
      ...(draft.maxTurns ? { max_turns: draft.maxTurns } : {}), ...(draft.effort ? { effort: draft.effort } : {}),
      ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}),
    },
    parse: parseAgentsReport,
  })
}

export function deleteAgent(client: ApiClient, name: string, scope: 'user' | 'project', conversationId?: string | null): Promise<AgentsReport> {
  return client.request({
    path: `/v1/settings/agents/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, method: 'DELETE',
    query: { scope, ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}) },
    parse: parseAgentsReport,
  })
}

export type OutputStylesReport = { styles: Array<{ key: string; name: string; description: string; source: string }>; active: string; activeLayer: string | null }

export function parseOutputStyles(value: unknown): OutputStylesReport {
  const data = rec(value)
  return {
    styles: list(data.styles, (item) => {
      const style = rec(item)
      return str(style.key) ? { key: str(style.key), name: str(style.name), description: str(style.description), source: str(style.source) } : null
    }),
    active: str(data.active, 'default') || 'default',
    activeLayer: optStr(data.active_layer),
  }
}

export function getOutputStyles(client: ApiClient, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<OutputStylesReport> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/output-styles', query: query(conversationId), parse: parseOutputStyles })
}

export function chooseOutputStyle(client: ApiClient, layer: 'user' | 'local', style: string, conversationId?: string | null): Promise<OutputStylesReport> {
  return client.request({
    path: '/v1/settings/output-styles', method: 'PUT',
    body: { layer, style, ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}) },
    parse: parseOutputStyles,
  })
}

export type KeybindingsFile = { path: string; text: string | null; error: string | null }
export type KeybindingsFiles = { orin: KeybindingsFile; claude: KeybindingsFile }

export function parseKeybindingsFiles(value: unknown): KeybindingsFiles {
  const data = rec(value)
  const file = (item: unknown): KeybindingsFile => {
    const entry = rec(item)
    return { path: str(entry.path), text: typeof entry.text === 'string' ? entry.text : null, error: optStr(entry.error) }
  }
  return { orin: file(data.orin), claude: file(data.claude) }
}

export function getKeybindingsFiles(client: ApiClient): Promise<KeybindingsFiles> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/keybindings', parse: parseKeybindingsFiles })
}

export function saveKeybindings(client: ApiClient, text: string): Promise<KeybindingsFiles> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/keybindings', method: 'PUT', body: { text }, parse: parseKeybindingsFiles })
}

export function getTrustedFolders(client: ApiClient): Promise<string[]> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/trusted-folders', parse: (value) => strings(rec(value).folders) })
}

export function revokeTrustedFolder(client: ApiClient, path: string): Promise<string[]> {
  return client.request({ path: '/v1/settings/trusted-folders/revoke', method: 'POST', body: { path }, parse: (value) => strings(rec(value).folders) })
}
```

Create `frontend/tests/unit/w7ApiClients.test.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { ApiClient } from '../../src/api/client'
import { ApiError } from '../../src/api/errors'
import {
  changePermissionRule, deleteAgent, getEffectiveSettings, getKeybindingsFiles, parseAgentsReport, parseHooksReport,
  saveAgent, saveHooks,
} from '../../src/api/settings'
import { enqueueMessage, getContext, listTasks, rewindCode, runStatusLine, stopTask } from '../../src/api/sessionTools'

type Call = { url: string; method: string; body: unknown; headers: Headers }

function client(responses: Array<{ status?: number; body: unknown }>) {
  const calls: Call[] = []
  const fetchImpl = (async (url: string, init: NonNullable<Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]>) => {
    calls.push({ url, method: init.method ?? 'GET', body: init.body ? JSON.parse(String(init.body)) : undefined, headers: new Headers(init.headers) })
    const next = responses.shift() ?? { body: {} }
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(next.body), { status: next.status ?? 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch
  return { api: new ApiClient({ fetchImpl, maxAttempts: 1, csrfToken: 'csrf' }), calls }
}

describe('W7 settings client', () => {
  it('reads effective settings with per-rule provenance and keeps only known layers', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = client([{ body: {
      project_root: 'C:/p', trusted: true, values: { model: 'x' }, provenance: { model: 'user', bad: 3 },
      layers: [{ name: 'user', file: 'C:/u.json', data: {} }, { name: 'evil', data: {} }],
      files: { user: [{ path: 'C:/u.json', exists: true }] }, writable: { user: 'C:/u.json', project: null, local: 'C:/l.json' },
      permissions: { allow: [{ rule: 'Read', layer: 'managed', file: 'm.json', duplicate: false }, { rule: '', layer: 'user' }] },
      errors: [{ file: 'x', message: 'bad' }], untrusted_ignored: ['hooks'],
    } }])
    const settings = await getEffectiveSettings(api, 'chat_1')
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe('/v1/settings/effective?conversation_id=chat_1')
    expect(settings.layers.map((layer) => layer.name)).toEqual(['user'])
    expect(settings.provenance).toEqual({ model: 'user' })
    expect(settings.permissions.allow).toEqual([{ rule: 'Read', layer: 'managed', file: 'm.json', duplicate: false }])
    expect(settings.permissions.deny).toEqual([])
    expect(settings.writable).toEqual({ user: 'C:/u.json', project: null, local: 'C:/l.json' })
  })

  it('sends a rule change and surfaces the server problems on a 422', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = client([
      { body: { permissions: { deny: [{ rule: 'WebFetch', layer: 'user', file: null, duplicate: false }] } } },
      { status: 422, body: { error: { code: 'invalid_rule', details: { problems: ["'Bash(' is not a permission rule"] } } } },
    ])
    const rules = await changePermissionRule(api, { layer: 'user', kind: 'deny', rule: 'WebFetch', op: 'add' })
    expect(rules.deny[0].rule).toBe('WebFetch')
    expect(calls[0]).toMatchObject({ method: 'PUT', body: { layer: 'user', kind: 'deny', rule: 'WebFetch', op: 'add' } })
    expect(calls[0].headers.get('X-CSRF-Token')).toBe('csrf')
    const failure = await changePermissionRule(api, { layer: 'user', kind: 'allow', rule: 'Bash(', op: 'add' }).catch((error) => error)
    expect(failure).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError)
    expect((failure as ApiError).problems).toEqual(["'Bash(' is not a permission rule"])
  })

  it('round-trips hooks, agents and keybindings shapes', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = client([
      { body: { disabled: false, effective: [{ event: 'PreToolUse', matcher: 'Bash', type: 'command', source: 'user', label: 'echo', timeout: 60 }], editable: { user: { file: 'u', hooks: { PreToolUse: [] } }, evil: {} } } },
      { body: { agents: [{ name: 'reviewer', description: 'd', origin: 'user', editable: true, prompt: 'p', tools: ['Read'], disallowed_tools: [], max_turns: 5 }] } },
      { body: { agents: [] } },
      { body: { orin: { path: 'k.json', text: '[]', error: null }, claude: { path: 'c.json', text: null, error: 'unreadable: OSError' } } },
    ])
    const hooks = await saveHooks(api, 'user', { PreToolUse: [] })
    expect(Object.keys(hooks.editable)).toEqual(['user'])
    expect(hooks.effective[0]).toMatchObject({ event: 'PreToolUse', matcher: 'Bash', timeout: 60 })
    const agents = await saveAgent(api, 'reviewer', { scope: 'user', description: 'd', prompt: 'p', tools: ['Read'], disallowedTools: [], model: null, permissionMode: null, maxTurns: 5, effort: null }, 'chat_1')
    expect(calls[1].body).toEqual({ scope: 'user', description: 'd', prompt: 'p', tools: ['Read'], disallowed_tools: [], max_turns: 5, conversation_id: 'chat_1' })
    expect(agents.agents[0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'reviewer', tools: ['Read'], maxTurns: 5, model: null })
    await deleteAgent(api, 'reviewer', 'user')
    expect(calls[2]).toMatchObject({ method: 'DELETE', url: '/v1/settings/agents/reviewer?scope=user' })
    const files = await getKeybindingsFiles(api)
    expect(files.claude).toEqual({ path: 'c.json', text: null, error: 'unreadable: OSError' })
  })

  it('parsers drop malformed entries', () => {
    expect(parseHooksReport({ effective: 'nope', editable: [] })).toEqual({ disabled: false, effective: [], editable: {}, errors: [] })
    expect(parseAgentsReport({ agents: [{ description: 'no name' }, 3] }).agents).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe('W7 session tools client', () => {
  it('queues, reads the context, stops a task and runs the status line', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = client([
      { body: { queued: true, queue_id: 'q1' } },
      { body: { available: true, limit_tokens: 1000, used_tokens: 250, percentage: 25, categories: [{ key: 'tools', tokens: 200 }, { key: 'free', tokens: -5 }] } },
      { body: { tasks: [{ task_id: 'shell:1', kind: 'shell', pid: 1, command: 'npm run dev', state: 'running', stoppable: true }, { task_id: 'x', kind: 'agent' }] } },
      { body: { stopped: true, tasks: [] } },
      { body: { enabled: true, lines: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'f'], error: null } },
    ])
    expect(await enqueueMessage(api, 'chat_1', 'next')).toEqual({ queued: true, queueId: 'q1', turnId: null })
    expect(calls[0].headers.get('Idempotency-Key')).toBeTruthy()
    const context = await getContext(api, 'chat_1')
    expect(context.categories).toEqual([{ key: 'tools', tokens: 200 }, { key: 'free', tokens: 0 }])
    const tasks = await listTasks(api, 'chat_1')
    expect(tasks).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(tasks[0]).toMatchObject({ taskId: 'shell:1', state: 'running', stoppable: true })
    expect((await stopTask(api, 'chat_1', 'shell:1')).stopped).toBe(true)
    expect(calls[3].url).toBe('/v1/conversations/chat_1/tasks/shell%3A1/stop')
    expect((await runStatusLine(api, 'chat_1')).lines).toHaveLength(5)
  })

  it('reads a blocked code rewind as a 409 with its files', async () => {
    const { api } = client([{ status: 409, body: { error: { code: 'rewind_blocked', category: 'CONFLICT', details: { files: [{ path: 'big.bin' }] } } } }])
    const failure = await rewindCode(api, 'chat_1', 'm1').catch((error) => error)
    expect(failure).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError)
    expect((failure as ApiError).code).toBe('rewind_blocked')
  })
})
```

```python
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { ApiError, parseApiError } from '../../src/api/errors'

describe('W7: an API error keeps what the server named as wrong', () => {
  it('reads details.problems from a 422', () => {

    expect(error.problems).toHaveLength(50)
    expect(error.problems.every((item) => item.length === 500)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('is empty without details, and absent from the public details', () => {
    const error = parseApiError(404, { error: { code: 'resource_not_found' } })
    expect(error.problems).toEqual([])
    expect(new ApiError({ status: 500 }).problems).toEqual([])
    expect('problems' in error.toPublicDetails()).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

### Task 9: The keybinding engine

`features/keybindings`: `keys.ts` (Claude Code's key syntax: modifiers, aliases — `meta`/`opt` are Alt, `win`/`super` are Cmd —, chords of up to three keystrokes with 3 s between them; on a Mac, Option+letter is read from the physical key), `catalog.ts` (the documented contexts; the actions Orin implements with browser and desktop-app defaults — a browser keeps `ctrl+t/w/n/l/tab` for itself, so those have no browser default —; the documented actions Orin does not have; reserved keys), `bindings.ts` (reads `keybindings.json` — a list or `{ bindings }`, `null` unbinds — with errors and warnings, and resolves defaults < Claude Code's file < Orin's file), `engine.tsx` (a stateful matcher; `KeybindingsProvider` loads both files from the settings API — a 404 on a shared server keeps the defaults —; `useKeybindingHandlers(context, handlers, enabled)` sends an action to the newest surface handling it, Global last; without a provider the browser defaults still work). `App` wraps everything in the provider.
