Create `frontend/src/features/keybindings/bindings.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * Reading `keybindings.json` and resolving what each key does (W7).
 *
 * The file is a list of `{ context, bindings: { key: action | null } }` blocks, or an object with that list under
 * `bindings` (and optional `$schema`/`$docs`). `null` unbinds a default. Later sources win: Orin's defaults, then
 * Claude Code's file (`~/.claude/keybindings.json`), then Orin's (`~/.orin/keybindings.json`).
 */
import { ACTIONS, BROWSER_RESERVED_KEYS, CLAUDE_CONTEXTS, type Environment, type KeyContext, RESERVED_KEYS, UNSUPPORTED_ACTIONS } from './catalog'
import { parseKeys } from './keys'

export type UserBinding = { context: KeyContext; keys: string[]; action: string | null }
export type Validation = { bindings: UserBinding[]; errors: string[]; warnings: string[] }

const MAX_BINDINGS = 500

export function validateKeybindings(text: string | null, environment: Environment): Validation {
  const result: Validation = { bindings: [], errors: [], warnings: [] }
  if (text === null || !text.trim()) return result
  let data: unknown
  try {
    data = JSON.parse(text)
  } catch (error) {
    result.errors.push(`não é JSON válido: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`.slice(0, 300))
    return result
  }
  const blocks = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (typeof data === 'object' && data !== null ? (data as Record<string, unknown>).bindings : undefined)
  if (!Array.isArray(blocks)) {
    result.errors.push("o arquivo precisa ser uma lista de blocos, ou um objeto com a lista em 'bindings'")
    return result
  }
  const seen = new Map<string, string>()
  blocks.forEach((block, index) => {
    const entry = typeof block === 'object' && block !== null ? (block as Record<string, unknown>) : null
    const where = `bloco ${index + 1}`
    if (!entry) { result.errors.push(`${where}: não é um objeto`); return }
    const context = entry.context
    if (typeof context !== 'string' || !(CLAUDE_CONTEXTS as readonly string[]).includes(context)) {
      result.errors.push(`${where}: contexto desconhecido '${String(context).slice(0, 60)}'`)
      return
    }
    const map = entry.bindings
    if (typeof map !== 'object' || map === null || Array.isArray(map)) {
      result.errors.push(`${where} (${context}): 'bindings' precisa ser um objeto de tecla → ação`)
      return
    }
    for (const [rawKey, action] of Object.entries(map as Record<string, unknown>)) {
      if (result.bindings.length >= MAX_BINDINGS) { result.errors.push(`mais de ${MAX_BINDINGS} atalhos; o resto foi ignorado`); return }
      const parsed = parseKeys(rawKey)
      if (!parsed.ok) { result.errors.push(`${context}: ${parsed.error}`); continue }
      const label = parsed.keys.join(' ')
      if (action !== null && typeof action !== 'string') { result.errors.push(`${context} ${label}: a ação precisa ser um texto ou null`); continue }
      if (parsed.keys.some((key) => RESERVED_KEYS.has(key))) { result.errors.push(`${context} ${label}: tecla reservada (copiar, colar e edição de texto)`); continue }
      if (typeof action === 'string' && !ACTIONS[action]) {
        if (UNSUPPORTED_ACTIONS.has(action)) result.warnings.push(`${context} ${label}: '${action}' não existe na interface do Orin; o atalho é ignorado`)
        else result.errors.push(`${context} ${label}: ação desconhecida '${action.slice(0, 60)}'`)
        continue
      }
      if (typeof action === 'string' && ACTIONS[action].context !== context && context !== 'Global') {
        result.warnings.push(`${context} ${label}: '${action}' pertence ao contexto ${ACTIONS[action].context}`)
      }
      if (environment === 'browser' && parsed.keys.some((key) => BROWSER_RESERVED_KEYS.has(key))) {
        result.warnings.push(`${context} ${label}: o navegador não deixa a página usar essa tecla; funciona só no app de desktop`)
      }
      const id = `${context}\u0000${label}`
      if (seen.has(id)) result.warnings.push(`${context} ${label}: atalho repetido; vale o último (${String(action)})`)
      seen.set(id, String(action))
      result.bindings.push({ context: context as KeyContext, keys: parsed.keys, action: action as string | null })
    }
  })
  return result
}

/** context → key sequence (canonical, space-joined) → action. */
export type Keymap = Map<KeyContext, Map<string, string>>

export function defaultKeymap(environment: Environment): Keymap {
  const map: Keymap = new Map()
  for (const [action, spec] of Object.entries(ACTIONS)) {
    const keys = environment === 'electron' ? spec.electron : spec.browser
    for (const key of keys) {
      const parsed = parseKeys(key)
      if (!parsed.ok) continue
      if (!map.has(spec.context)) map.set(spec.context, new Map())
      map.get(spec.context)!.set(parsed.keys.join(' '), action)
    }
  }
  return map
}

/** Defaults, then each file's bindings in order (later wins); `null` removes a key. */
export function resolveKeymap(environment: Environment, files: Array<UserBinding[]>): Keymap {
  const map = defaultKeymap(environment)
  for (const bindings of files) {
    for (const binding of bindings) {
      if (!map.has(binding.context)) map.set(binding.context, new Map())
      const sequence = binding.keys.join(' ')
      if (binding.action === null) map.get(binding.context)!.delete(sequence)
      else map.get(binding.context)!.set(sequence, binding.action)
    }
  }
  return map
}

/** The keys bound to an action in the resolved keymap (for hints and the help screen). */
export function keysFor(map: Keymap, action: string): string[][] {
  const context = ACTIONS[action]?.context
  const found: string[][] = []
  for (const [ctx, bindings] of map) {
    if (context && ctx !== context && ctx !== 'Global') continue
    for (const [sequence, bound] of bindings) if (bound === action) found.push(sequence.split(' '))
  }
  return found
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/keybindings/catalog.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * Contexts and actions of Claude Code's `keybindings.json` (its public documentation, 2026-10-20), and the defaults Orin
 * uses in a browser and in the desktop app. A browser keeps some keys for itself (`ctrl+t`, `ctrl+w`, `ctrl+n`,
 * `ctrl+tab`, `ctrl+l`, …), so those actions have no browser default.
 */

export const CLAUDE_CONTEXTS = [
  'Global', 'Chat', 'Autocomplete', 'Settings', 'Confirmation', 'Tabs', 'Help', 'Transcript', 'HistorySearch', 'Task',
  'ThemePicker', 'Attachments', 'Footer', 'MessageSelector', 'DiffDialog', 'DiffPanel', 'ModelPicker', 'EffortSlider',
  'Select', 'Plugin', 'AbovePrompt', 'AbovePromptInput', 'AbovePromptSelect', 'Pane', 'PaneField', 'Agents', 'Scroll',
] as const
export type KeyContext = (typeof CLAUDE_CONTEXTS)[number]

export type ActionSpec = { context: KeyContext; label: string; browser: string[]; electron: string[] }

const both = (context: KeyContext, label: string, ...keys: string[]): ActionSpec => ({ context, label, browser: keys, electron: keys })

/** What Orin's interface does on each action (the rest of Claude Code's actions are recognized and reported). */
export const ACTIONS: Record<string, ActionSpec> = {
  'app:toggleTranscript': both('Global', 'Abrir ou fechar a transcrição detalhada', 'ctrl+o'),
  'app:toggleTodos': { context: 'Global', label: 'Mostrar ou esconder as tarefas do agente', browser: [], electron: ['ctrl+t'] },
  'chat:submit': both('Chat', 'Enviar a mensagem', 'enter'),
  'chat:newline': both('Chat', 'Nova linha', 'shift+enter', 'ctrl+j'),
  'chat:cancel': both('Chat', 'Interromper o agente', 'escape'),
  'chat:cycleMode': both('Chat', 'Trocar o modo de permissão', 'shift+tab'),
  'chat:modelPicker': both('Chat', 'Escolher o modelo', 'alt+p'),
  'chat:thinkingToggle': both('Chat', 'Ligar ou desligar o raciocínio estendido', 'alt+t'),
  'chat:sendNow': both('Chat', 'Enviar agora (interrompe e envia)', 'ctrl+enter'),
  'chat:clearInput': { context: 'Chat', label: 'Limpar o campo de mensagem', browser: [], electron: ['ctrl+l'] },
  'history:search': both('Chat', 'Buscar no histórico de mensagens', 'ctrl+r'),
  'history:previous': both('Chat', 'Mensagem anterior do histórico', 'up'),
  'history:next': both('Chat', 'Próxima mensagem do histórico', 'down'),
  'autocomplete:accept': both('Autocomplete', 'Aceitar a sugestão', 'tab'),
  'autocomplete:dismiss': both('Autocomplete', 'Fechar as sugestões', 'escape'),
  'autocomplete:previous': both('Autocomplete', 'Sugestão anterior', 'up'),
  'autocomplete:next': both('Autocomplete', 'Próxima sugestão', 'down'),
  'confirm:yes': both('Confirmation', 'Confirmar', 'enter'),
  'confirm:no': both('Confirmation', 'Recusar', 'escape'),
  'confirm:previous': both('Confirmation', 'Opção anterior', 'up'),
  'confirm:next': both('Confirmation', 'Próxima opção', 'down'),
  'transcript:toggleShowAll': both('Transcript', 'Mostrar tudo na transcrição', 'ctrl+e'),
  'transcript:exit': both('Transcript', 'Fechar a transcrição', 'escape', 'q'),
  'historySearch:next': both('HistorySearch', 'Próximo resultado', 'ctrl+r'),
  'historySearch:accept': both('HistorySearch', 'Usar o resultado', 'tab'),
  'historySearch:cancel': both('HistorySearch', 'Cancelar a busca', 'escape'),
  'historySearch:execute': both('HistorySearch', 'Enviar o resultado', 'enter'),
  'select:next': both('Select', 'Próximo item', 'down'),
  'select:previous': both('Select', 'Item anterior', 'up'),
  'select:accept': both('Select', 'Escolher', 'enter'),
  'select:cancel': both('Select', 'Cancelar', 'escape'),
  'tabs:next': both('Tabs', 'Próxima aba', 'right'),
  'tabs:previous': both('Tabs', 'Aba anterior', 'left'),
  'help:dismiss': both('Help', 'Fechar a ajuda', 'escape'),
  'settings:search': both('Settings', 'Buscar nas configurações', '/'),
  'diff:dismiss': both('DiffDialog', 'Fechar o diff', 'escape'),
  'diff:previousFile': both('DiffDialog', 'Arquivo anterior', 'up'),
  'diff:nextFile': both('DiffDialog', 'Próximo arquivo', 'down'),
  'modelPicker:decreaseEffort': both('ModelPicker', 'Menos esforço', 'left'),
  'modelPicker:increaseEffort': both('ModelPicker', 'Mais esforço', 'right'),
  'effortSlider:decreaseEffort': both('EffortSlider', 'Menos esforço', 'left'),
  'effortSlider:increaseEffort': both('EffortSlider', 'Mais esforço', 'right'),
}

/** Claude Code actions Orin's interface does not have (a binding to one is kept but reported as a warning). */
export const UNSUPPORTED_ACTIONS = new Set([
  'app:interrupt', 'app:exit', 'app:redraw', 'app:toggleReplTab', 'app:toggleDiffNoiseFilter', 'app:toggleDiffPreSession',
  'app:cycleDiffBase', 'app:diffFileListUp', 'app:diffFileListDown', 'chat:clearScreen', 'chat:killAgents', 'chat:fastMode',
  'chat:queueSubmit', 'chat:undo', 'chat:externalEditor', 'chat:stash', 'chat:imagePaste', 'abovePrompt:toggle', 'abovePrompt:focus',
  'voice:pushToTalk', 'task:background', 'confirm:nextField', 'confirm:previousField', 'confirm:toggle', 'confirm:cycleMode',
  'permission:toggleDebug', 'historySearch:cycleScope', 'diff:previousSource', 'diff:nextSource', 'diff:back', 'scroll:pageUp',
  'scroll:pageDown', 'scroll:fullPageUp', 'scroll:fullPageDown', 'scroll:top', 'scroll:bottom', 'scroll:lineUp', 'scroll:lineDown',
  'scroll:halfPageUp', 'scroll:halfPageDown', 'selection:copy', 'selection:clear', 'selection:extendLeft', 'selection:extendRight',
  'selection:extendUp', 'selection:extendDown', 'selection:extendLineStart', 'selection:extendLineEnd',
  'theme:toggleSyntaxHighlighting', 'attachments:next', 'attachments:previous', 'attachments:remove', 'attachments:exit',
  'footer:next', 'footer:previous', 'footer:up', 'footer:down', 'footer:openSelected', 'footer:clearSelection', 'footer:close',
  'footer:dismiss', 'modelPicker:thisSessionOnly', 'effortSlider:toggleUltracode', 'effortSlider:thisSessionOnly',
  'select:pageUp', 'select:pageDown', 'select:first', 'select:last', 'plugin:toggle', 'plugin:install', 'plugin:favorite',
  'abovePrompt:next', 'abovePrompt:previous', 'abovePrompt:press', 'abovePrompt:leave', 'abovePrompt:highlightNext',
  'abovePrompt:highlightPrevious', 'pane:scrollUp', 'pane:scrollDown', 'pane:pageUp', 'pane:pageDown', 'pane:top',
  'pane:bottom', 'pane:grow', 'pane:shrink', 'pane:close', 'pane:next', 'pane:previous', 'settings:retry',
  'agents:switchView', 'agents:togglePin', 'agents:find', 'agents:rename', 'agents:previousGroup', 'agents:nextGroup',
])

/** Keys no binding may take: copy, paste and the text editing every field relies on (Claude Code reserves ctrl+c/ctrl+d). */
export const RESERVED_KEYS = new Set(['ctrl+c', 'ctrl+d', 'ctrl+v', 'ctrl+x', 'ctrl+a', 'ctrl+z', 'cmd+c', 'cmd+v', 'cmd+x', 'cmd+a', 'cmd+z', 'ctrl+m', 'ctrl+i', 'ctrl+h', 'ctrl+['])

/** Keys a browser keeps for itself: they work only in the desktop app. */
export const BROWSER_RESERVED_KEYS = new Set([
  'ctrl+t', 'ctrl+w', 'ctrl+n', 'ctrl+tab', 'ctrl+shift+tab', 'ctrl+shift+t', 'ctrl+shift+n', 'ctrl+shift+w', 'ctrl+l',
  'ctrl+q', 'alt+f4', 'f11', 'cmd+t', 'cmd+w', 'cmd+n', 'cmd+q', 'cmd+l', 'ctrl+pageup', 'ctrl+pagedown',
])

export type Environment = 'browser' | 'electron'
```

Create `frontend/src/features/keybindings/engine.tsx` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * The keybinding engine (W7): which action a key press means in the contexts on screen, chords included, and a hook
 * for surfaces to handle their actions. Text fields keep typing: a handler decides what it takes.
 */
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { ApiClient } from '../../api/client'
import { getKeybindingsFiles } from '../../api/settings'
import { defaultKeymap, type Keymap, resolveKeymap, validateKeybindings, type Validation } from './bindings'
import type { Environment, KeyContext } from './catalog'
import { CHORD_TIMEOUT_MS, strokeFromEvent } from './keys'

export const PENDING = '__chord_pending__'

export type KeyMatch = string | typeof PENDING | null

/** A stateful matcher over a keymap: feed it key events, it answers an action, PENDING (inside a chord) or null. */
export function createMatcher(keymap: Keymap, clock: () => number = () => Date.now()) {
  let pending: { sequence: string; at: number } | null = null
  function lookup(sequence: string, contexts: readonly KeyContext[]): KeyMatch {
    let prefix = false
    for (const context of contexts) {
      const bindings = keymap.get(context)
      if (!bindings) continue
      const action = bindings.get(sequence)
      if (action) return action
      for (const key of bindings.keys()) if (key.startsWith(`${sequence} `)) prefix = true
    }
    return prefix ? PENDING : null
  }
  return function match(event: Parameters<typeof strokeFromEvent>[0], contexts: readonly KeyContext[]): KeyMatch {
    const stroke = strokeFromEvent(event)
    if (!stroke) return null
    const now = clock()
    if (pending && now - pending.at <= CHORD_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      const continued = lookup(`${pending.sequence} ${stroke}`, contexts)
      if (continued === PENDING) { pending = { sequence: `${pending.sequence} ${stroke}`, at: now }; return PENDING }
      pending = null
      if (continued) return continued
    }
    pending = null
    const single = lookup(stroke, contexts)
    if (single === PENDING) pending = { sequence: stroke, at: now }
    return single
  }
}

export function detectEnvironment(): Environment {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'orinDesktop' in window ? 'electron' : 'browser'
}

type Handler = (event: KeyboardEvent) => boolean | void
type Registration = { id: number; context: KeyContext; handlers: Record<string, Handler> }

type KeybindingsValue = {
  environment: Environment
  keymap: Keymap
  report: { orin: Validation; claude: Validation }
  match: (event: KeyboardEvent, contexts: readonly KeyContext[]) => KeyMatch
  register: (registration: Omit<Registration, 'id'>) => () => void
  reload: () => void
}

const empty: Validation = { bindings: [], errors: [], warnings: [] }

/** Registrations plus the window listener that sends each key to the newest surface handling its action. */
function createDispatcher(initial: ReturnType<typeof createMatcher>) {
  let matcher = initial
  let registrations: Registration[] = []
  let nextId = 1
  function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (event.defaultPrevented || event.isComposing) return
    // The newest surface first (a dialog over the chat), Global last.
    const stack = [...registrations].reverse()
    const contexts = [...new Set([...stack.map((item) => item.context).filter((context) => context !== 'Global'), 'Global' as const])]
    const action = matcher(event, contexts)
    if (action === PENDING) { event.preventDefault(); return }
    if (!action) return
    for (const item of stack) {
      const handler = item.handlers[action]
      if (handler && handler(event) !== false) { event.preventDefault(); return }
    }
  }
  return {
    setMatcher(next: ReturnType<typeof createMatcher>) { matcher = next },
    register(registration: Omit<Registration, 'id'>) {
      const entry = { ...registration, id: nextId++ }
      registrations = [...registrations, entry]
      return () => { registrations = registrations.filter((item) => item.id !== entry.id) }
    },
    attach() {
      window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown)
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown)
    },
  }
}

const KeybindingsContext = createContext<KeybindingsValue | null>(null)

export function KeybindingsProvider({ client, environment = detectEnvironment(), children }: { client?: ApiClient; environment?: Environment; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [report, setReport] = useState<{ orin: Validation; claude: Validation }>({ orin: empty, claude: empty })
  const [generation, setGeneration] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!client) return
    let alive = true
    // The files live on the host: a shared server answers 404 and the defaults stay (never an error for the person).
    getKeybindingsFiles(client).then((files) => {
      if (!alive) return
      setReport({ orin: validateKeybindings(files.orin.text, environment), claude: validateKeybindings(files.claude.text, environment) })
    }).catch(() => undefined)
    return () => { alive = false }
  }, [client, environment, generation])
  const keymap = useMemo(
    () => resolveKeymap(environment, [report.claude.bindings, report.orin.bindings]),
    [environment, report],
  )
  const matcher = useMemo(() => createMatcher(keymap), [keymap])
  const [dispatcher] = useState(() => createDispatcher(matcher))
  useEffect(() => { dispatcher.setMatcher(matcher) }, [dispatcher, matcher])
  useEffect(() => dispatcher.attach(), [dispatcher])
  const register = useCallback((registration: Omit<Registration, 'id'>) => dispatcher.register(registration), [dispatcher])
  const value = useMemo<KeybindingsValue>(() => ({
    environment, keymap, report, register, match: matcher, reload: () => setGeneration((value) => value + 1),
  }), [environment, keymap, report, register, matcher])
  return <KeybindingsContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</KeybindingsContext.Provider>
}

// Without a provider (a surface rendered on its own, as in a test), the browser defaults still work: one shared
// dispatcher attaches its listener the first time something registers.
const fallback: KeybindingsValue = (() => {
  const keymap = defaultKeymap('browser')
  const match = createMatcher(keymap)
  const dispatcher = createDispatcher(match)
  let attached = false
  return {
    environment: 'browser', keymap, report: { orin: empty, claude: empty }, match, reload: () => undefined,
    register: (registration) => {
      if (!attached && typeof window !== 'undefined') { dispatcher.attach(); attached = true }
      return dispatcher.register(registration)
    },
  }
})()

export function useKeybindings(): KeybindingsValue {
  return useContext(KeybindingsContext) ?? fallback
}

/** Handle some actions of one context while `enabled`; a handler returning false lets the key through. */
export function useKeybindingHandlers(context: KeyContext, handlers: Record<string, Handler>, enabled = true) {
  const { register } = useKeybindings()
  const latest = useRef(handlers)
  useEffect(() => { latest.current = handlers })
  const actions = Object.keys(handlers).sort().join('|')
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return
    const proxy: Record<string, Handler> = {}
    for (const action of actions.split('|').filter(Boolean)) proxy[action] = (event) => latest.current[action]?.(event)
    return register({ context, handlers: proxy })
  }, [context, actions, enabled, register])
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/keybindings/keys.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * Key strings in Claude Code's `keybindings.json` syntax (W7): modifiers joined with `+` (`ctrl+shift+k`), chords
 * separated by a space (`ctrl+k ctrl+s`). `alt`, `opt`, `option` and `meta` are the Alt/Option key; `cmd`, `command`,
 * `super` and `win` are the Command/Windows key. Names are case-insensitive; the canonical form orders the modifiers
 * ctrl, alt, shift, cmd.
 */

const MODIFIER_ALIASES: Record<string, 'ctrl' | 'alt' | 'shift' | 'cmd'> = {
  ctrl: 'ctrl', control: 'ctrl',
  alt: 'alt', opt: 'alt', option: 'alt', meta: 'alt',
  shift: 'shift',
  cmd: 'cmd', command: 'cmd', super: 'cmd', win: 'cmd',
}
const MODIFIER_ORDER = ['ctrl', 'alt', 'shift', 'cmd'] as const

const KEY_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  esc: 'escape', escape: 'escape', return: 'enter', enter: 'enter', tab: 'tab', space: 'space', ' ': 'space',
  up: 'up', arrowup: 'up', down: 'down', arrowdown: 'down', left: 'left', arrowleft: 'left', right: 'right', arrowright: 'right',
  pageup: 'pageup', pagedown: 'pagedown', home: 'home', end: 'end', backspace: 'backspace', delete: 'delete', del: 'delete',
  insert: 'insert', plus: '+', minus: '-',
}
const NAMED_KEYS = new Set([
  'escape', 'enter', 'tab', 'space', 'up', 'down', 'left', 'right', 'pageup', 'pagedown', 'home', 'end', 'backspace', 'delete',
  'insert', ...Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, index) => `f${index + 1}`),
])

export const CHORD_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000

export type KeyParse = { ok: true; keys: string[] } | { ok: false; error: string }

/** One keystroke (`ctrl+shift+k`) in canonical form, or null when it is not a valid keystroke. */
export function normalizeStroke(raw: string): string | null {
  const text = raw.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!text) return null
  // A trailing "+" is the plus key itself ("ctrl++").
  const parts = text.endsWith('++') ? [...text.slice(0, -2).split('+'), '+'] : text.split('+')
  if (parts.some((part) => part === '')) return null
  const key = parts[parts.length - 1]
  const modifiers = new Set<string>()
  for (const part of parts.slice(0, -1)) {
    const modifier = MODIFIER_ALIASES[part]
    if (!modifier || modifiers.has(modifier)) return null
    modifiers.add(modifier)
  }
  const name = KEY_ALIASES[key] ?? key
  if (!NAMED_KEYS.has(name) && [...name].length !== 1) return null
  return [...MODIFIER_ORDER.filter((modifier) => modifiers.has(modifier)), name].join('+')
}

/** A whole binding: one keystroke or a chord of up to three. */
export function parseKeys(raw: string): KeyParse {
  const strokes = raw.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)
  if (strokes.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'empty key' }
  if (strokes.length > 3) return { ok: false, error: 'a chord has at most three keystrokes' }
  const keys: string[] = []
  for (const stroke of strokes) {
    const normalized = normalizeStroke(stroke)
    if (!normalized) return { ok: false, error: `'${stroke}' is not a keystroke` }
    keys.push(normalized)
  }
  return { ok: true, keys }
}

/** The canonical keystroke of a keyboard event, or null for a lone modifier press. */
export function strokeFromEvent(event: Pick<KeyboardEvent, 'key' | 'ctrlKey' | 'altKey' | 'shiftKey' | 'metaKey'> & { code?: string }): string | null {
  // Option+letter on a Mac types a symbol (Option+P is "π"): with Alt held, the physical key names the letter.
  const physical = event.altKey ? /^Key([A-Z])$/.exec(event.code ?? '')?.[1] ?? /^Digit([0-9])$/.exec(event.code ?? '')?.[1] : undefined
  const raw = physical ? physical.toLowerCase() : event.key
  if (!raw || ['Control', 'Shift', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'AltGraph', 'CapsLock', 'Dead', 'Unidentified'].includes(raw)) return null
  let name = KEY_ALIASES[raw.toLowerCase()] ?? raw.toLowerCase()
  // Shift turns "/" into "?" and "1" into "!": the shifted character is the key, so "shift" is implied, not added,
  // for printable characters (Claude Code's own syntax binds "?" directly).
  const printable = [...name].length === 1 && name !== ' '
  if (name === ' ') name = 'space'
  const modifiers: string[] = []
  if (event.ctrlKey) modifiers.push('ctrl')
  if (event.altKey) modifiers.push('alt')
  if (event.shiftKey && (!printable || /[a-z]/.test(name))) modifiers.push('shift')
  if (event.metaKey) modifiers.push('cmd')
  if (printable && /[a-z]/.test(name)) name = name.toLowerCase()
  return [...modifiers, name].join('+')
}

export function displayKeys(keys: readonly string[], platform: 'mac' | 'other' = 'other'): string {
  const label = (stroke: string) => stroke.split('+').map((part) => {
    if (part === 'ctrl') return platform === 'mac' ? '⌃' : 'Ctrl'
    if (part === 'alt') return platform === 'mac' ? '⌥' : 'Alt'
    if (part === 'shift') return platform === 'mac' ? '⇧' : 'Shift'
    if (part === 'cmd') return platform === 'mac' ? '⌘' : 'Win'
    if (part === 'escape') return 'Esc'
    if (part === 'enter') return 'Enter'
    if (part === 'space') return 'Espaço'
    if (part.length === 1) return part.toUpperCase()
    return part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1)
  }).join(platform === 'mac' ? '' : '+')
  return keys.map(label).join(' ')
}
```

Create `frontend/tests/unit/w7Keybindings.test.tsx` (it must not exist yet):

```python
import { act, render } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { defaultKeymap, keysFor, resolveKeymap, validateKeybindings } from '../../src/features/keybindings/bindings'
import { createMatcher, KeybindingsProvider, PENDING, useKeybindingHandlers } from '../../src/features/keybindings/engine'
import { displayKeys, normalizeStroke, parseKeys, strokeFromEvent } from '../../src/features/keybindings/keys'

const key = (key: string, extra: Partial<KeyboardEvent> & { code?: string } = {}) =>
  ({ key, ctrlKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false, metaKey: false, ...extra })

describe('key syntax', () => {
  it('normalizes modifiers, aliases and chords', () => {
    expect(normalizeStroke('Shift+Ctrl+K')).toBe('ctrl+shift+k')
    expect(normalizeStroke('meta+p')).toBe('alt+p')
    expect(normalizeStroke('opt+P')).toBe('alt+p')
    expect(normalizeStroke('win+e')).toBe('cmd+e')
    expect(normalizeStroke('esc')).toBe('escape')
    expect(normalizeStroke('ctrl++')).toBe('ctrl++')
    expect(normalizeStroke('ctrl+hyper+k')).toBeNull()
    expect(normalizeStroke('ctrl+ctrl+k')).toBeNull()
    expect(parseKeys('ctrl+k ctrl+s')).toEqual({ ok: true, keys: ['ctrl+k', 'ctrl+s'] })
    expect(parseKeys('a b c d').ok).toBe(false)
  })

  it('reads keyboard events like the file names keys', () => {
    expect(strokeFromEvent(key('Enter', { shiftKey: true }))).toBe('shift+enter')
    expect(strokeFromEvent(key('K', { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true }))).toBe('ctrl+shift+k')
    expect(strokeFromEvent(key('?', { shiftKey: true }))).toBe('?')
    expect(strokeFromEvent(key('π', { altKey: true, code: 'KeyP' }))).toBe('alt+p')
    expect(strokeFromEvent(key('Shift', { shiftKey: true }))).toBeNull()
    expect(displayKeys(['ctrl+k', 'ctrl+s'])).toBe('Ctrl+K Ctrl+S')
  })
})

describe('keybindings.json', () => {
  it('accepts both shapes, unbinds with null and resolves later files over earlier ones', () => {
    const claude = validateKeybindings(JSON.stringify([{ context: 'Chat', bindings: { 'ctrl+k ctrl+s': 'chat:submit' } }]), 'browser')
    const orin = validateKeybindings(JSON.stringify({ $schema: 'x', bindings: [{ context: 'Chat', bindings: { enter: null, 'ctrl+k ctrl+s': 'chat:newline' } }] }), 'browser')
    expect(claude.errors).toEqual([])
    expect(orin.errors).toEqual([])
    const map = resolveKeymap('browser', [claude.bindings, orin.bindings])
    expect(map.get('Chat')!.get('enter')).toBeUndefined()
    expect(map.get('Chat')!.get('ctrl+k ctrl+s')).toBe('chat:newline')
    expect(keysFor(map, 'chat:submit')).toEqual([])
  })

  it('reports errors and warnings without throwing', () => {
    const result = validateKeybindings(JSON.stringify([
      { context: 'Nowhere', bindings: {} },
      { context: 'Chat', bindings: { 'ctrl+c': 'chat:submit', 'ctrl+t': 'chat:submit', 'alt+x': 'chat:fastMode', 'alt+y': 'chat:explode', 'hyper+z': 'chat:submit', 'alt+q': 3 } },
    ]), 'browser')
    expect(result.errors.some((item) => item.includes('Nowhere'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.errors.some((item) => item.includes('reservada'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.errors.some((item) => item.includes('chat:explode'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.errors.some((item) => item.includes('hyper+z'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.warnings.some((item) => item.includes('chat:fastMode'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.warnings.some((item) => item.includes('navegador'))).toBe(true)
    expect(result.bindings.map((item) => item.keys.join(' '))).toEqual(['ctrl+t'])
    expect(validateKeybindings('{oops', 'browser').errors).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(validateKeybindings('{"bindings": 3}', 'browser').errors).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('has different defaults in a browser and in the desktop app', () => {
    expect(defaultKeymap('electron').get('Global')!.get('ctrl+t')).toBe('app:toggleTodos')
    expect(defaultKeymap('browser').get('Global')!.get('ctrl+t')).toBeUndefined()
    expect(defaultKeymap('browser').get('Global')!.get('ctrl+o')).toBe('app:toggleTranscript')
  })
})

describe('matcher', () => {
  it('waits inside a chord and gives up after the timeout', () => {
    let now = 0
    const map = resolveKeymap('browser', [[{ context: 'Chat', keys: ['ctrl+k', 'ctrl+s'], action: 'chat:submit' }]])
    const match = createMatcher(map, () => now)
    expect(match(key('k', { ctrlKey: true }), ['Chat'])).toBe(PENDING)
    expect(match(key('s', { ctrlKey: true }), ['Chat'])).toBe('chat:submit')
    expect(match(key('k', { ctrlKey: true }), ['Chat'])).toBe(PENDING)
    now = 3500
    expect(match(key('s', { ctrlKey: true }), ['Chat'])).toBeNull()
    expect(match(key('Escape'), ['Autocomplete', 'Chat'])).toBe('autocomplete:dismiss')
  })
})

describe('provider', () => {
  it('sends an action to the newest surface that handles it', () => {
    const calls: string[] = []
    function Surface({ name }: { name: string }) {
      useKeybindingHandlers('Global', { 'app:toggleTranscript': () => { calls.push(name) } })
      return null
    }
    function Dialog() {
      useKeybindingHandlers('Confirmation', { 'confirm:no': () => { calls.push('dialog') } })
      return null
    }
    render(<KeybindingsProvider environment="browser"><Surface name="chat" /><Dialog /></KeybindingsProvider>)
    act(() => { window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'o', ctrlKey: true })) })
    act(() => { window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Escape' })) })
    expect(calls).toEqual(['chat', 'dialog'])
  })
})
```

### Task 10: Built-in slash commands in the chat

`features/commands/builtins.ts` lists spec §10.1's commands with Claude Code's names and aliases (decision 8: `/ultraplan` is left out — Claude Code removed it), and the excluded ones. `useChatCommands` holds what each does in a chat; a built-in is offered only once it has a handler (decision 7), and Batch B implements those that need nothing new: `/help`, `/clear` (`/reset`, `/new`), `/exit`, `/compact [instructions]`, `/copy [N]`, `/export [file]`, `/model`, `/plan [description]`, `/config`, `/mcp`, `/plugin`, `/skills`, `/memory`, `/theme`, `/init`, `/review`, `/security-review` (Orin's own prompts), `/sandbox`, `/add-dir <folder>`, `/version`, `/release-notes`, `/status`. The `/` picker shows them first (an alias finds its command), then the folder's own skills and commands (`GET .../commands`), then plugins and library skills; picking a built-in without arguments runs it at once. A typed built-in runs in the interface (even while a turn runs); an excluded one says so and is not sent; anything else is sent as before. The composer's keys go through the engine's Chat context (`chat:submit`, `chat:newline` — `ctrl+j` inserts a line too —, `chat:cancel`, `chat:cycleMode`, `chat:sendNow`, `chat:modelPicker`, `chat:clearInput`); the picker's arrows, Tab and Escape through the Autocomplete context (Enter still picks). `/help` and the info commands answer in a dialog (Escape: the Help context). The model picker opens on a signal.

Create `frontend/src/features/commands/CommandInfoDialog.tsx` (it must not exist yet):

```python
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { keysFor } from '../keybindings/bindings'
import { ACTIONS } from '../keybindings/catalog'
import { useKeybindingHandlers, useKeybindings } from '../keybindings/engine'
import { displayKeys } from '../keybindings/keys'
import type { BuiltinCommand } from './builtins'
import type { InfoPanel } from './useChatCommands'

const SHORTCUTS = [
  'chat:submit', 'chat:newline', 'chat:cancel', 'chat:cycleMode', 'chat:modelPicker', 'chat:sendNow', 'history:search',
  'app:toggleTranscript', 'app:toggleTodos',
]

/** `/help` (commands and keys) and the short answers of `/status`, `/version`, `/sandbox`. */
export function CommandInfoDialog({ panel, commands, onClose }: { panel: InfoPanel; commands: readonly BuiltinCommand[]; onClose: () => void }) {
  const closeRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const { keymap } = useKeybindings()
  useKeybindingHandlers('Help', { 'help:dismiss': () => onClose() })
  useEffect(() => { closeRef.current?.focus() }, [])
  const title = panel.kind === 'help' ? 'Comandos e atalhos' : panel.title
  return (
    <div className="command-info__backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose() }}>
      <section className="command-info" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="command-info-title">
        <header className="command-info__header">
          <h2 id="command-info-title">{title}</h2>
          <button ref={closeRef} type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={onClose}>Fechar</button>
        </header>
        {panel.kind === 'text' ? (
          <div className="command-info__body" role="region" aria-label={title} tabIndex={0}>
            {panel.lines.map((line, index) => <p key={index}>{line}</p>)}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="command-info__body command-info__body--help" role="region" aria-label="Lista de comandos e atalhos" tabIndex={0}>
            <h3>Comandos</h3>
            <dl className="command-info__list">
              {commands.map((command) => (
                <div key={command.name} className="command-info__row">
                  <dt>/{command.name}{command.argumentHint ? ` ${command.argumentHint}` : ''}{command.aliases.length > 0 ? <span className="command-info__aliases"> ({command.aliases.map((alias) => `/${alias}`).join(', ')})</span> : null}</dt>
                  <dd>{command.description}</dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
            <h3>Atalhos</h3>
            <dl className="command-info__list">
              {SHORTCUTS.map((action) => {
                const keys = keysFor(keymap, action)
                if (keys.length === 0) return null
                return (
                  <div key={action} className="command-info__row">
                    <dt><kbd>{keys.map((sequence) => displayKeys(sequence)).join(' · ')}</kbd></dt>
                    <dd>{ACTIONS[action]?.label ?? action}</dd>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </dl>
            <p className="command-info__note">Digite <kbd>/</kbd> para ver os comandos, <kbd>@</kbd> para citar arquivos.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/commands/builtins.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * Built-in slash commands with Claude Code's names and aliases (spec §10.1). The interface runs them itself; nothing
 * is sent to the model unless the command says so. A command is offered only once the chat implements its handler,
 * so the picker never lists something that does nothing.
 */

export type BuiltinCategory = 'session' | 'config' | 'info' | 'flow'

export type BuiltinCommand = {
  name: string
  aliases: string[]
  description: string
  argumentHint?: string
  category: BuiltinCategory
}

const c = (name: string, category: BuiltinCategory, description: string, aliases: string[] = [], argumentHint?: string): BuiltinCommand =>
  ({ name, aliases, description, category, argumentHint })

export const BUILTIN_COMMANDS: readonly BuiltinCommand[] = [
  // Session and context
  c('add-dir', 'session', 'Adiciona uma pasta de trabalho a esta sessão', [], '<pasta>'),
  c('agents', 'session', 'Gerencia os agentes definidos em arquivos'),
  c('autocompact', 'session', 'Mostra ou muda a compactação automática', [], '[on|off]'),
  c('btw', 'session', 'Pergunta paralela que não entra no histórico', [], '<pergunta>'),
  c('clear', 'session', 'Começa uma conversa nova', ['reset', 'new']),
  c('compact', 'session', 'Resume a conversa para liberar contexto', [], '[instruções]'),
  c('context', 'session', 'Mostra o uso do contexto em grade'),
  c('copy', 'session', 'Copia a última resposta', [], '[N]'),
  c('cd', 'session', 'Muda a pasta de trabalho da sessão', [], '<pasta>'),
  c('diff', 'session', 'Mostra as mudanças no diretório de trabalho'),
  c('export', 'session', 'Exporta a conversa como texto', [], '[arquivo]'),
  c('rename', 'session', 'Renomeia a conversa', ['name'], '<nome>'),
  c('resume', 'session', 'Retoma uma conversa anterior', ['continue']),
  c('rewind', 'session', 'Volta o código e/ou a conversa a uma mensagem anterior', ['checkpoint', 'undo']),
  c('branch', 'session', 'Ramifica a conversa e muda para o ramo', [], '[nome]'),
  c('fork', 'session', 'Copia a conversa para uma nova sessão e fica nesta', [], '[prompt]'),
  c('subtask', 'session', 'Delega uma tarefa a um subagente que herda a conversa', [], '<tarefa>'),
  c('exit', 'session', 'Sai da sessão', ['quit']),
  // Configuration
  c('config', 'config', 'Abre as configurações', ['settings']),
  c('doctor', 'config', 'Verifica a instalação do Orin'),
  c('effort', 'config', 'Muda o nível de esforço', [], '[low|medium|high|xhigh|max]'),
  c('fast', 'config', 'Liga ou desliga o modo rápido', [], '[on|off]'),
  c('model', 'config', 'Escolhe o modelo'),
  c('output-style', 'config', 'Escolhe o estilo de resposta', [], '[estilo]'),
  c('permissions', 'config', 'Gerencia as regras de permissão', ['allowed-tools']),
  c('hooks', 'config', 'Mostra e edita os hooks'),
  c('mcp', 'config', 'Gerencia os servidores MCP'),
  c('plugin', 'config', 'Gerencia os plugins', ['plugins', 'marketplace']),
  c('reload-plugins', 'config', 'Recarrega os plugins'),
  c('reload-skills', 'config', 'Recarrega as skills'),
  c('skills', 'config', 'Lista as skills'),
  c('memory', 'config', 'Edita os arquivos de memória'),
  c('pause-memory', 'config', 'Pausa ou retoma o aprendizado da memória', ['memory-pause', 'toggle-memory']),
  c('keybindings', 'config', 'Edita os atalhos de teclado'),
  c('statusline', 'config', 'Configura a linha de status', [], '[descrição]'),
  c('theme', 'config', 'Muda o tema de cores'),
  c('color', 'config', 'Muda a cor da barra de mensagem', [], '[cor|default]'),
  c('auto-mode-setup', 'config', 'Rascunha o ambiente do modo automático'),
  c('import', 'config', 'Importa configurações de outra ferramenta'),
  c('init', 'config', 'Cria o ORIN.md do projeto'),
  // Information
  c('help', 'info', 'Mostra os comandos e os atalhos'),
  c('status', 'info', 'Mostra a versão, o modelo, a pasta e as configurações'),
  c('tasks', 'info', 'Mostra os processos e agentes em segundo plano', ['bashes']),
  c('usage', 'info', 'Mostra o custo e o uso da sessão', ['cost', 'stats']),
  c('version', 'info', 'Mostra a versão do Orin'),
  c('release-notes', 'info', 'Mostra as notas da versão'),
  c('insights', 'info', 'Relatório das sessões recentes'),
  c('skill-doctor', 'info', 'Mostra o custo de contexto de cada skill'),
  c('sandbox', 'info', 'Explica o isolamento dos comandos'),
  // Flow
  c('plan', 'flow', 'Entra no Modo Plano', [], '[descrição]'),
  c('loop', 'flow', 'Repete um prompt enquanto a sessão estiver aberta', ['proactive'], '[intervalo] [prompt]'),
  c('loops', 'flow', 'Mostra as repetições ativas'),
  c('review', 'flow', 'Revisa as mudanças', [], '[alvo]'),
  c('security-review', 'flow', 'Revisão de segurança das mudanças'),
  c('ultrareview', 'flow', 'Revisão completa em vários agentes'),
  c('restart', 'flow', 'Reinicia o Orin', ['update']),
  c('desktop', 'flow', 'Abre esta sessão no app de desktop', ['app']),
]

/** Claude Code commands Orin leaves out (accounts, cloud, terminal-only): typing one says so instead of sending it. */
export const EXCLUDED_COMMANDS = new Set([
  'login', 'logout', 'voice', 'mobile', 'chrome', 'ide', 'install-github-app', 'install-slack-app', 'teleport', 'remote-env',
  'session', 'cloud-plugins', 'artifacts', 'autofix-pr', 'privacy-settings', 'passes', 'stickers', 'radio', 'powerup',
  'wellbeing', 'advisor', 'focus', 'upgrade', 'usage-credits', 'extra-usage', 'pro-trial-expired', 'rate-limit-options',
  'setup-bedrock', 'setup-vertex', 'feedback', 'bug', 'heapdump', 'team-onboarding', '__remote-workflow',
  'workflow-launch-exec', 'tui', 'terminal-setup', 'scroll-speed', 'ultraplan',
])

const BY_NAME = new Map<string, BuiltinCommand>()
for (const command of BUILTIN_COMMANDS) {
  BY_NAME.set(command.name, command)
  for (const alias of command.aliases) BY_NAME.set(alias, command)
}

export function findBuiltin(name: string): BuiltinCommand | null {
  return BY_NAME.get(name.trim().toLowerCase()) ?? null
}

export function isExcludedCommand(name: string): boolean {
  const clean = name.trim().toLowerCase()
  return EXCLUDED_COMMANDS.has(clean) || clean.startsWith('design-')
}

/** `/name rest` → `{ name, args }` for a message that is a slash command; null otherwise. */
export function parseSlash(text: string): { name: string; args: string } | null {
  const match = /^\/([A-Za-z0-9_:.-]+)(?:\s+([\s\S]*))?$/.exec(text.trim())
  return match ? { name: match[1], args: (match[2] ?? '').trim() } : null
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/commands/useChatCommands.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * What each built-in slash command does in a chat (W7). Commands that need a surface of a later batch are added with
 * that surface; until then they are not offered (`offered` lists only what has a handler here).
 */
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import type { ApiClient } from '../../api/client'
import { ApiError } from '../../api/errors'
import { CODE_PERMISSION_MODES } from '../../api/codeMode'
import { addDirectory, compactConversation, getInstance } from '../../api/sessionTools'
import { BUILTIN_COMMANDS, type BuiltinCommand, findBuiltin, isExcludedCommand, parseSlash } from './builtins'

export type InfoPanel =
  | { kind: 'help' }
  | { kind: 'text'; title: string; lines: string[] }

export type ChatCommandDeps = {
  client: ApiClient
  conversationId: string
  /** The conversation's messages, oldest first (for /copy and /export). */
  messages: ReadonlyArray<{ role: string; content: string }>
  title: string
  modelId: string
  provider: string
  permissionMode: string
  mode: string
  workspacePath: string | null
  navigate: (path: string) => void
  /** Sends text as a message of this conversation. */
  send: (text: string) => Promise<void>
  reload: () => Promise<void>
  notify: (text: string) => void
  openInfo: (panel: InfoPanel) => void
  openPreferences: () => void
  openModelPicker: () => void
  setPermissionMode: (mode: 'plan_mode') => Promise<void> | void
  copyText?: (text: string) => Promise<void>
  download?: (name: string, text: string) => void
}

type Handler = (args: string) => Promise<void> | void

// Orin's own words for the prompt commands (not Claude Code's prompts).
export const INIT_PROMPT = [
  'Analise este repositório e crie (ou atualize) o arquivo ORIN.md na raiz com o que um agente precisa saber para trabalhar aqui:',
  'como instalar, rodar e testar; a estrutura das pastas; as convenções de código; e cuidados específicos do projeto.',
  'Seja conciso e factual; não invente comandos que você não verificou.',
].join(' ')
export const REVIEW_PROMPT = 'Revise as mudanças atuais do repositório (git diff, incluindo arquivos novos): aponte bugs, riscos, testes que faltam e melhorias, em ordem de gravidade, com arquivo e linha.'
export const SECURITY_REVIEW_PROMPT = 'Faça uma revisão de segurança das mudanças atuais do repositório: injeção, autenticação e autorização, segredos expostos, validação de entrada, dependências e dados sensíveis. Liste cada achado com gravidade, arquivo, linha e correção sugerida.'
export const SANDBOX_TEXT = [
  'O Orin não isola os comandos numa sandbox do sistema operacional.',
  'No perfil local, os comandos de shell rodam com as permissões da sua conta, limitados pelas regras de permissão e pelo modo de permissão da sessão.',
  'Num Orin compartilhado (modo servidor), shell, MCP por stdio e hooks de plugins ficam desligados.',
]

function lastAssistant(messages: ChatCommandDeps['messages'], nth: number): string | null {
  const answers = messages.filter((item) => item.role === 'assistant' && item.content.trim())
  return answers[answers.length - nth]?.content ?? null
}

function transcript(deps: ChatCommandDeps): string {
  const lines = [`# ${deps.title || 'Conversa do Orin'}`, '']
  for (const item of deps.messages) {
    if (!item.content.trim()) continue
    lines.push(item.role === 'user' ? 'Você:' : 'Orin:', item.content.trim(), '')
  }
  return lines.join('\n')
}

function defaultDownload(name: string, text: string) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }))
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = name
  link.click()
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000)
}

export function chatCommandHandlers(deps: ChatCommandDeps): Record<string, Handler> {
  const copy = deps.copyText ?? ((text: string) => navigator.clipboard.writeText(text))
  const download = deps.download ?? defaultDownload
  const home = () => deps.navigate('/')
  return {
    help: () => deps.openInfo({ kind: 'help' }),
    clear: home,
    exit: home,
    compact: async (args) => {
      await compactConversation(deps.client, deps.conversationId, args || undefined)
      deps.notify('Compactando a conversa…')
      await deps.reload()
    },
    copy: async (args) => {
      const nth = /^\d+$/.test(args) ? Math.max(1, Number(args)) : 1
      const text = lastAssistant(deps.messages, nth)
      if (!text) { deps.notify('Não há resposta para copiar.'); return }
      await copy(text)
      deps.notify(nth === 1 ? 'Última resposta copiada.' : `Resposta ${nth} copiada.`)
    },
    export: (args) => {
      const name = (args.trim() || `orin-${deps.conversationId}.txt`).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
      download(name.endsWith('.txt') || name.endsWith('.md') ? name : `${name}.txt`, transcript(deps))
      deps.notify('Conversa exportada.')
    },
    model: () => deps.openModelPicker(),
    plan: async (args) => {
      await deps.setPermissionMode('plan_mode')
      deps.notify('Modo Plano ativado.')
      if (args) await deps.send(args)
    },
    config: () => deps.navigate('/settings/general'),
    mcp: () => deps.navigate('/settings/mcp'),
    plugin: () => deps.navigate('/settings/plugins'),
    skills: () => deps.navigate('/settings/skills'),
    memory: () => deps.navigate('/settings/memory'),
    theme: () => deps.openPreferences(),
    init: () => deps.send(INIT_PROMPT),
    review: (args) => deps.send(args ? `${REVIEW_PROMPT}\nFoco: ${args}` : REVIEW_PROMPT),
    'security-review': () => deps.send(SECURITY_REVIEW_PROMPT),
    sandbox: () => deps.openInfo({ kind: 'text', title: 'Isolamento dos comandos', lines: SANDBOX_TEXT }),
    'add-dir': async (args) => {
      if (!args) { deps.notify('Diga a pasta: /add-dir <pasta>'); return }
      try {
        const added = await addDirectory(deps.client, deps.conversationId, args)
        deps.notify(`Pasta adicionada: ${added.path}`)
      } catch (error) {
        deps.notify(error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 404
          ? 'Adicionar pastas não está disponível neste Orin.'
          : 'Não foi possível adicionar a pasta. Confira o caminho.')
      }
    },
    version: async () => {
      const instance = await getInstance(deps.client)
      deps.openInfo({ kind: 'text', title: 'Versão', lines: [`Orin ${instance.version}`] })
    },
    'release-notes': async () => {
      const instance = await getInstance(deps.client)
      window.open(`https://github.com/carlos-edu2367/orin/releases/tag/v${encodeURIComponent(instance.version)}`, '_blank', 'noopener')
    },
    status: async () => {
      const instance = await getInstance(deps.client).catch(() => null)
      deps.openInfo({
        kind: 'text', title: 'Status da sessão',
        lines: [
          `Orin ${instance?.version ?? '—'} (${instance?.mode === 'server' ? 'servidor' : 'local'})`,
          `Modelo: ${deps.provider ? `${deps.provider} · ` : ''}${deps.modelId || '—'}`,
          `Modo: ${deps.mode === 'code' ? 'Orin Code' : 'Chat'} · permissões: ${CODE_PERMISSION_MODES.find((item) => item.mode === deps.permissionMode)?.label ?? (deps.permissionMode || '—')}`,
          `Pasta: ${deps.workspacePath ?? 'pasta gerenciada pelo Orin'}`,
        ],
      })
    },
  }
}

export type SlashOutcome = { kind: 'handled' } | { kind: 'excluded'; name: string } | { kind: 'message' }

export function useChatCommands(deps: ChatCommandDeps) {
  const handlers = useMemo(() => chatCommandHandlers(deps), [deps])
  const offered = useMemo<BuiltinCommand[]>(() => BUILTIN_COMMANDS.filter((command) => command.name in handlers), [handlers])
  async function run(text: string): Promise<SlashOutcome> {
    const parsed = parseSlash(text)
    if (!parsed) return { kind: 'message' }
    const builtin = findBuiltin(parsed.name)
    if (builtin && handlers[builtin.name]) {
      await handlers[builtin.name](parsed.args)
      return { kind: 'handled' }
    }
    if (!builtin && isExcludedCommand(parsed.name)) return { kind: 'excluded', name: parsed.name }
    // A skill, a plugin's command or an MCP prompt: the server expands it (and a built-in not offered yet is sent too).
    return { kind: 'message' }
  }
  return { offered, run }
}
```

```python
const KIND_LABEL: Record<SlashItem['kind'], string> = { builtin: 'Orin', command: 'Comando', skill: 'Skill' }

/** Every entry the picker offers for a query, built-ins first. Exported for the composer, which must know the same. */
export function slashItems(
  query: string,
  commands: readonly PluginCommand[],
  skills: readonly SkillSummary[] = [],
  builtins: readonly BuiltinCommand[] = [],
  catalog: readonly CatalogCommand[] = [],
): SlashItem[] {
  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase()
  const builtinNames = new Set(builtins.flatMap((item) => [item.name, ...item.aliases]))
  const items: SlashItem[] = [
    ...builtins.map((item) => ({ id: `builtin:${item.name}`, token: item.name, kind: 'builtin' as const, description: item.description, hint: item.argumentHint, aliases: item.aliases })),
    ...catalog.filter((item) => !builtinNames.has(item.name)).map((item) => ({
      id: `disk:${item.name}`, token: item.name, kind: item.kind === 'command' ? 'command' as const : 'skill' as const,
      description: item.description, hint: item.argumentHint || undefined,
    })),
    ...commands.map((item) => ({
      id: item.command_id,
      token: commandToken(item),
      kind: 'command' as const,
      description: item.description,
      hint: item.argument_hint,
    })),
    ...skills.filter((skill) => skill.available).map((skill) => ({
      id: `skill:${skill.id}`,
      token: skillToken(skill, [...commands]),
      kind: 'skill' as const,
      description: skill.description,
    })),
  ]
  return items

}
```

```python
    if (event.nativeEvent.isComposing) return

    if (pickerKeys) return

    const handled = (() => {
      switch (action) {
        case 'chat:cycleMode':
          if (!(effectiveCodeMode === 'code' || Boolean(sessionContext) || Boolean(onCodePermissionModeChange))) return false
          stepPermissionMode()
          return true
        case 'chat:cancel':
          if (!running || !onStop) return false
          onStop()
          return true
        case 'chat:submit':
          if (running || !canSend) return true
          if (value.trim() || attachments.length > 0) onSubmit()
          return true
        case 'chat:newline':
          // Shift+Enter already types a line break; any other key bound to it inserts one.
          if (event.key === 'Enter' && event.shiftKey) return false
          insertAtCaret('\n')
          return true
        case 'chat:sendNow':
          if (!value.trim()) return true
          if (running && onSendNow) onSendNow()
          else if (!running && canSend) onSubmit()
          return true
        case 'chat:modelPicker':
          if (!onOpenModelPicker) return false
          onOpenModelPicker()
          return true
        case 'chat:clearInput':
          onChange('')
          return true
        default:
          return false
      }
    })()
```

Create `frontend/tests/unit/w7SlashCommands.test.tsx` (it must not exist yet):

```python
import { fireEvent, render, renderHook, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { ApiClient } from '../../src/api/client'
import type { ProviderModel } from '../../src/api/providers'
import { ModelPicker } from '../../src/components/ModelPicker'
import { BUILTIN_COMMANDS, findBuiltin, isExcludedCommand, parseSlash } from '../../src/features/commands/builtins'
import { CommandInfoDialog } from '../../src/features/commands/CommandInfoDialog'
import { chatCommandHandlers, INIT_PROMPT, type ChatCommandDeps, useChatCommands } from '../../src/features/commands/useChatCommands'
import { Composer } from '../../src/features/conversations/Composer'
import { slashItems } from '../../src/features/conversations/CommandPicker'

function fakeClient(responses: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string; body: unknown }> = []
  const fetchImpl = (async (url: string, init: NonNullable<Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]>) => {
    calls.push({ url, method: init.method ?? 'GET', body: init.body ? JSON.parse(String(init.body)) : undefined })
    const key = Object.keys(responses).find((item) => url.includes(item))
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(key ? responses[key] : {}), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch
  return { client: new ApiClient({ fetchImpl, maxAttempts: 1 }), calls }
}

function deps(overrides: Partial<ChatCommandDeps> = {}): ChatCommandDeps {
  return {
    client: fakeClient({}).client, conversationId: 'chat_1',
    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'oi' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'primeira' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'segunda' }],
    title: 'Teste', modelId: 'm', provider: 'openrouter', permissionMode: 'ask_permissions', mode: 'code', workspacePath: 'C:/p',
    navigate: vi.fn(), send: vi.fn(async () => undefined), reload: vi.fn(async () => undefined), notify: vi.fn(), openInfo: vi.fn(),
    openPreferences: vi.fn(), openModelPicker: vi.fn(), setPermissionMode: vi.fn(), copyText: vi.fn(async () => undefined), download: vi.fn(),
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('built-in commands', () => {
  it('find a command by name or alias and parse its arguments', () => {
    expect(findBuiltin('reset')?.name).toBe('clear')
    expect(findBuiltin('allowed-tools')?.name).toBe('permissions')
    expect(parseSlash('/compact  focus on tests ')).toEqual({ name: 'compact', args: 'focus on tests' })
    expect(parseSlash('/caminho/arquivo')).toBeNull()
    expect(parseSlash('texto /compact')).toBeNull()
    expect(isExcludedCommand('login') && isExcludedCommand('design-review') && !isExcludedCommand('help')).toBe(true)
    expect(new Set(BUILTIN_COMMANDS.map((command) => command.name)).size).toBe(BUILTIN_COMMANDS.length)
  })

  it('run what is offered, refuse what Orin excludes and send everything else', async () => {
    const value = deps()
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useChatCommands(value))
    expect(result.current.offered.some((command) => command.name === 'help')).toBe(true)
    expect(result.current.offered.some((command) => command.name === 'rewind')).toBe(false) // its surface comes later
    expect(await result.current.run('/reset')).toEqual({ kind: 'handled' })
    expect(value.navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/')
    expect(await result.current.run('/login')).toEqual({ kind: 'excluded', name: 'login' })
    expect(await result.current.run('/my-skill arg')).toEqual({ kind: 'message' })
    expect(await result.current.run('não é comando')).toEqual({ kind: 'message' })
  })

  it('handlers do what their commands say', async () => {
    const { client, calls } = fakeClient({ '/compact': { turn_id: 't1' }, '/v1/instance': { version: '0.9.4', mode: 'local', capabilities: {} } })
    const value = deps({ client })
    const handlers = chatCommandHandlers(value)
    await handlers.copy('2')
    expect(value.copyText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('primeira')
    await handlers.compact('foque nos testes')
    expect(calls.find((call) => call.url.endsWith('/compact'))?.body).toEqual({ instructions: 'foque nos testes' })
    expect(value.reload).toHaveBeenCalled()
    handlers.export('nota')
    expect(value.download).toHaveBeenCalledWith('nota.txt', expect.stringContaining('Orin:\nsegunda'))
    await handlers.plan('desenhe a migração')
    expect(value.setPermissionMode).toHaveBeenCalledWith('plan_mode')
    expect(value.send).toHaveBeenCalledWith('desenhe a migração')
    await handlers.init('')
    expect(value.send).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(INIT_PROMPT)
    await handlers.status('')
    expect(value.openInfo).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'text', lines: expect.arrayContaining([expect.stringContaining('0.9.4')]) }))
    await handlers['add-dir']('')
    expect(value.notify).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('Diga a pasta: /add-dir <pasta>')
  })
})

describe('picker and composer', () => {
  it('lists built-ins first and finds them by alias', () => {
    const offered = BUILTIN_COMMANDS.filter((command) => ['clear', 'help'].includes(command.name))
    const items = slashItems('reset', [], [], offered, [{ name: 'deploy', description: 'Deploy', kind: 'command', origin: 'user', argumentHint: '', context: null }])
    expect(items.map((item) => item.token)).toEqual(['clear'])
    expect(slashItems('', [], [], offered, [{ name: 'deploy', description: 'Deploy', kind: 'command', origin: 'user', argumentHint: '', context: null }])
      .map((item) => `${item.kind}:${item.token}`)).toEqual(['builtin:clear', 'builtin:help', 'command:deploy'])
  })

  it('sends now, clears and inserts a line through the keybinding engine', () => {
    const onSendNow = vi.fn()
    const onChange = vi.fn()
    const { rerender } = render(<Composer value="faça agora" onChange={onChange} onSubmit={vi.fn()} running onStop={vi.fn()} onSendNow={onSendNow} />)
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Mensagem')
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Enter', ctrlKey: true })
    expect(onSendNow).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    rerender(<Composer value="abc" onChange={onChange} onSubmit={vi.fn()} />)
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'j', ctrlKey: true })
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('abc\n')
  })
})

describe('the model picker opens on a signal and the help dialog lists keys', () => {
  it('opens the model list when the signal grows', () => {
    const models = [{ model_id: 'm1', display_name: 'Model 1', provider: 'openrouter', capabilities: [], input_modalities: [], output_modalities: [] }] as unknown as ProviderModel[]
    const props = { providers: ['openrouter' as const], provider: 'openrouter' as const, onProviderChange: vi.fn(), models, modelId: 'm1', onModelChange: vi.fn() }
    const { rerender } = render(<ModelPicker {...props} openSignal={0} />)
    expect(screen.queryByRole('listbox')).toBeNull()
    rerender(<ModelPicker {...props} openSignal={1} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('listbox')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('/help shows commands with aliases and the keys in force', () => {
    const onClose = vi.fn()
    render(<CommandInfoDialog panel={{ kind: 'help' }} commands={BUILTIN_COMMANDS.filter((command) => command.name === 'clear')} onClose={onClose} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Comandos e atalhos' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('/reset, /new', { exact: false })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('Ctrl+O')).toBeInTheDocument()
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: 'Escape' })
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
```

```python
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test'
import { CONVERSATION_ID, expectNoSeriousA11yViolations, mockChat } from '../support/w7Chat'

test.describe('W7 built-in slash commands', () => {
  test('the picker offers Orin commands first, then the folder’s own', async ({ page }) => {
    await mockChat(page)
    await page.goto(`/chats/${CONVERSATION_ID}`)
    const input = page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mensagem' })
    await input.fill('/')
    const picker = page.getByRole('listbox', { name: 'Comandos e skills' })
    await expect(picker).toBeVisible()
    await expect(picker.getByRole('option').first()).toContainText('/add-dir')
    await expect(picker.getByRole('option', { name: /\/deploy/ })).toContainText('Comando')
    await expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page)
    await input.fill('/reset')
    await expect(picker.getByRole('option')).toHaveCount(1)
    await expect(picker.getByRole('option')).toContainText('/clear')
  })

  test('/help lists the commands and the keys, and Escape closes it', async ({ page }) => {
    await mockChat(page)
    await page.goto(`/chats/${CONVERSATION_ID}`)
    const input = page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mensagem' })
    await input.fill('/help')
    // Enter picks the highlighted command; a built-in without arguments runs at once.
    await input.press('Enter')
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Comandos e atalhos' })
    await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
    await expect(dialog).toContainText('/compact')
    await expect(dialog).toContainText('Ctrl+O')
    await expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page)
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape')
    await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
    await expect(input).toHaveValue('')
  })

  test('/compact sends its instructions and /status answers with the version', async ({ page }) => {
    const sent = await mockChat(page, ({ method, path }) => (method === 'POST' && path.endsWith('/compact') ? { status: 202, body: { turn_id: 't2' } } : undefined))
    await page.goto(`/chats/${CONVERSATION_ID}`)
    const input = page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mensagem' })
    await input.fill('/compact foque nos testes')
    await input.press('Enter')
    await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Compactando a conversa' })).toBeVisible()
    expect(sent.find((item) => item.path.endsWith('/compact'))?.body).toEqual({ instructions: 'foque nos testes' })
    await input.fill('/status')
    await input.press('Enter')
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Status da sessão' })
    await expect(dialog).toContainText('Orin 0.9.4')
    await expect(dialog).toContainText('C:/Projetos/pagamentos')
  })

  test('a command Orin leaves out says so and is not sent', async ({ page }) => {
    const sent = await mockChat(page)
    await page.goto(`/chats/${CONVERSATION_ID}`)
    const input = page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mensagem' })
    await input.fill('/login')
    await input.press('Enter')
    await expect(page.getByText('/login não existe no Orin.')).toBeVisible()
    expect(sent.some((item) => item.method === 'POST' && item.path.endsWith('/messages'))).toBe(false)
    await expect(input).toHaveValue('/login')
  })
})
```

Create `frontend/tests/support/w7Chat.ts` (it must not exist yet):

```python
/**
 * W7 browser-test support: an idle Orin Code conversation behind a mocked `/v1` API, a theme, and an axe check.
 * Each spec adds its own routes through `extra` (matched first) and reads what was sent from `sent`.
 */
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright'
import { expect, type Page, type Route } from '@playwright/test'

export const CONVERSATION_ID = 'conv-w7'

export type SentRequest = { method: string; path: string; body: unknown }
export type ExtraRoute = (request: { method: string; path: string; body: unknown }) => { status?: number; body: unknown } | undefined

export const conversation = {
  conversation_id: CONVERSATION_ID,
  title: 'Refatorar o módulo de pagamentos',
  state: 'completed',
  provider: 'openrouter',
  model_id: 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5',
  mode: 'code',
  permission_mode: 'ask_permissions',
  project_id: null,
  workspace: { mode: 'local_folder', path: 'C:/Projetos/pagamentos', folder_name: 'pagamentos', status: 'available' },
  messages: [
    { message_id: 'm-user-1', turn_id: 't1', role: 'user', content: 'Explique o fluxo de cobrança.', status: 'completed', retryable: false, attachments: [], command: null },
    { message_id: 'm-asst-1', turn_id: 't1', role: 'assistant', content: 'O fluxo começa em `billing/charge.py`, que valida o pedido e chama o gateway.', status: 'completed', retryable: false, attachments: [], command: null },
  ],
  turns: [{ turn_id: 't1', state: 'completed', created_at: '2026-10-20T12:00:00.000Z', started_at: '2026-10-20T12:00:01.000Z', finished_at: '2026-10-20T12:00:09.000Z', scheduled_by_schedule_id: null, code_mode: 'code' }],
  activities: [],
  activity_cursor: '1',
}

async function fulfill(route: Route, body: unknown, status = 200) {
  await route.fulfill({ status, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify(body) })
}

export async function setTheme(page: Page, mode: 'dark' | 'light') {
  await page.addInitScript((value) => { window.localStorage.setItem('orin.theme.mode', value) }, mode)
}

export async function mockChat(page: Page, extra: ExtraRoute = () => undefined): Promise<SentRequest[]> {
  const sent: SentRequest[] = []
  await page.route('**/chats/**', async (route) => {
    if (route.request().resourceType() !== 'document') { await route.fallback(); return }
    const response = await route.fetch()
    const body = (await response.text()).replace('<head>', '<head><meta name="csrf-token" content="csrf-w7-e2e">')
    await route.fulfill({ response, body })
  })
  await page.route('**/v1/**', async (route) => {
    const request = route.request()
    const path = new URL(request.url()).pathname
    const method = request.method()
    let body: unknown
    try { body = request.postDataJSON() } catch { body = undefined }
    sent.push({ method, path, body })
    const custom = extra({ method, path, body })
    if (custom) { await fulfill(route, custom.body, custom.status ?? 200); return }
    if (path.endsWith('/events')) {
      await route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: 'text/event-stream', body: 'event: heartbeat\ndata: {"cursor":"1"}\n\n' })
      return
    }
    if (method === 'GET' && path === `/v1/conversations/${CONVERSATION_ID}`) { await fulfill(route, conversation); return }
    if (method === 'GET' && (path === '/v1/conversations' || path === '/v1/projects' || path === '/v1/projects/sidebar' || path === '/v1/skills' || path === '/v1/plugins/commands')) {
      await fulfill(route, { items: [] }); return
    }
    if (method === 'GET' && path === `/v1/conversations/${CONVERSATION_ID}/commands`) {
      await fulfill(route, { trusted: true, commands: [{ name: 'deploy', description: 'Publica a versão no ambiente de testes', kind: 'command', origin: 'project', argument_hint: '<ambiente>', context: null }] })
      return
    }
    if (method === 'GET' && path === '/v1/instance') { await fulfill(route, { version: '0.9.4', mode: 'local', capabilities: { host_folders: true }, credential: 'session' }); return }
    if (method === 'GET' && path === '/v1/settings/keybindings') {
      await fulfill(route, { orin: { path: 'C:/Users/p/.orin/keybindings.json', text: null, error: null }, claude: { path: 'C:/Users/p/.claude/keybindings.json', text: null, error: null } })
      return
    }
    await fulfill(route, {})
  })
  return sent
}

export async function expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page: Page, include?: string): Promise<void> {
  const builder = new AxeBuilder({ page })
  const results = await (include ? builder.include(include) : builder).analyze()
  const blocking = results.violations.filter((violation) => violation.impact === 'critical' || violation.impact === 'serious')
  expect(blocking, JSON.stringify(blocking, null, 2)).toEqual([])
}
```

## Batch C — Backend for the chat surfaces (Tasks 12–15)

**Every "Create" below is a new file: if a file of that name exists, stop and report — never overwrite it.**

### Decisions recorded while planning Batch C (the ledger repeats them)

13. **A fork copies only finished text messages** (no turns, attachments or tool steps), with the same model, mode, permission mode, project and folder; a managed workspace's files are not copied.
14. **A shell turn (`!command`) runs in bypass mode** because the person typed the command; deny rules, hooks and a policy that disables bypass still apply, and plan mode still refuses it. No model is called in that turn.
15. **Rewinding the conversation forks it before the message**: the interface opens the new conversation with the message in the composer; the original stays as it was (Claude Code rewinds in place).
16. **`@server:uri` counts only for a connected server's name**, at most five per message and 20 000 characters.

### Task 12: Conversations can be forked and renamed; a turn may be a shell turn (store and application)

`PostgresChatStore.fork(conversation_id, user_id, *, before_message_id=None, title=None)` creates a conversation with this one's finished messages — all of them, or those before one of the person's messages, which comes back as `prompt` — with the same model, mode, permission mode and project, no turns, attachments or tool steps (decision 13); a message that is not the person's, or another person's conversation, is not found. `rename` trims and refuses a blank title. `turn_kind` accepts `shell`; `ChatApplication.shell(user_id, conversation_id, command, key)` sends `!command` as a shell turn, only in a Code conversation (4000 characters at most).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/conversations/test_w7_fork_rename_shell.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/conversations/chat.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `PostgresChatStore.fork`, `.rename`; `ChatApplication.fork`, `.rename`, `.shell`; `turn_kind` `shell`.

Create `tests/unit/conversations/test_w7_fork_rename_shell.py` (it must not exist yet):

```python
"""W7: a conversation can be forked (whole, or before one of the person's messages) and renamed; `!command` makes a
shell turn in a Code conversation."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, update

from agentos.api.contracts import ApplicationNotFoundError
from agentos.conversations.chat import ChatApplication, PostgresChatStore
from agentos.persistence.postgres.schema import conversation_messages, conversation_turns, conversations
from agentos.provider_catalog.models import ProviderCatalogContext

from tests.unit.conversations.test_chat_application import _store


def _answered(store: PostgresChatStore, conversation_id: str, answer: str) -> None:
    with store._engine.begin() as c:
        c.execute(update(conversation_messages).where(
            conversation_messages.c.conversation_id == conversation_id, conversation_messages.c.status != "completed",
        ).values(status="completed", content=answer))
        c.execute(update(conversation_turns).where(conversation_turns.c.conversation_id == conversation_id).values(state="completed"))


def _conversation(store: PostgresChatStore, mode: str = "code") -> tuple[ChatApplication, str, list[str]]:
    application = ChatApplication(store)
    receipt = application.create(
        ProviderCatalogContext("user-1", "conversation.create"), message="primeira pergunta", provider="openrouter",
        model_id="model-a", workspace_id=None, idempotency_key="k1", code_mode=mode,
    )
    _answered(store, receipt.conversation_id, "primeira resposta")
    application.send("user-1", receipt.conversation_id, "segunda pergunta", "k2")
    _answered(store, receipt.conversation_id, "segunda resposta")
    with store._engine.connect() as c:
        ids = [row.message_id for row in c.execute(select(conversation_messages.c.message_id).where(
            conversation_messages.c.conversation_id == receipt.conversation_id).order_by(conversation_messages.c.sequence))]
    return application, receipt.conversation_id, ids


def _messages(store: PostgresChatStore, conversation_id: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    with store._engine.connect() as c:
        return [(row.role, row.content) for row in c.execute(select(conversation_messages).where(
            conversation_messages.c.conversation_id == conversation_id).order_by(conversation_messages.c.sequence))]


def test_a_whole_fork_copies_the_finished_messages_and_the_settings() -> None:
    store = _store()
    application, conversation_id, _ids = _conversation(store)
    fork = application.fork(conversation_id, "user-1")
    assert fork["messages"] == 4 and fork["prompt"] is None and fork["title"].endswith("(ramo)")
    assert _messages(store, fork["conversation_id"]) == _messages(store, conversation_id)
    with store._engine.connect() as c:
        original = c.execute(select(conversations).where(conversations.c.conversation_id == conversation_id)).mappings().one()
        copy = c.execute(select(conversations).where(conversations.c.conversation_id == fork["conversation_id"])).mappings().one()
    assert (copy["mode"], copy["permission_mode"], copy["model_id"], copy["state"]) == (original["mode"], original["permission_mode"], "model-a", "completed")


def test_a_fork_before_a_message_keeps_what_came_before_and_returns_the_message() -> None:
    store = _store()
    application, conversation_id, ids = _conversation(store)
    fork = application.fork(conversation_id, "user-1", before_message_id=ids[2], title="Voltar à segunda pergunta")
    assert fork["prompt"] == "segunda pergunta" and fork["title"] == "Voltar à segunda pergunta"
    assert _messages(store, fork["conversation_id"]) == [("user", "primeira pergunta"), ("assistant", "primeira resposta")]
    with pytest.raises(ApplicationNotFoundError):
        application.fork(conversation_id, "user-1", before_message_id=ids[1])  # an answer is not a rewind point
    with pytest.raises(ApplicationNotFoundError):
        application.fork(conversation_id, "someone-else")


def test_rename_trims_and_refuses_blank_or_foreign() -> None:
    store = _store()
    application, conversation_id, _ids = _conversation(store)
    assert application.rename(conversation_id, "user-1", "  Novo   nome ")["title"] == "Novo nome"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        application.rename(conversation_id, "user-1", "   ")
    with pytest.raises(ApplicationNotFoundError):
        application.rename(conversation_id, "someone-else", "x")


def test_a_shell_command_is_a_shell_turn_only_in_a_code_conversation() -> None:
    store = _store()
    application, conversation_id, _ids = _conversation(store)
    receipt = application.shell("user-1", conversation_id, "  git status  ", "k3")
    with store._engine.connect() as c:
        turn = c.execute(select(conversation_turns).where(conversation_turns.c.turn_id == receipt.turn_id)).mappings().one()
    assert turn["turn_kind"] == "shell"
    assert _messages(store, conversation_id)[-2] == ("user", "!git status")
    chat_application, chat_id, _ = _conversation(_store(), mode="chat")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        chat_application.shell("user-1", chat_id, "ls", "k4")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        application.shell("user-1", conversation_id, "   ", "k5")
```
